- 🛑🛑★★★★ [Name superseded hashes DEAD — omitting them is NOT enough](feedback-name-superseded-hashes-dead-not-merely-omitted.md) — **a VERIFIED artefact for a SUPERSEDED design is MORE dangerous than an unverified one: everything about it looks correct, INCLUDING ITS ASSERTION LOG, and a hash reported in a transcript OUTLIVES the artefact it names.** The instance: a real V92 cut, **182/182 assertions, from-disk verified**, carried the OLD rung map (`b4 = sign(gp-0x6abc)`, 110 B cave, no 427 `sar` fix), was superseded before flight and **never flew** — but its hashes stayed live in the transcript with a full passing log, and **the only tell was a `6ABC` token buried in the old filename.** ⇒ **On a re-cut, name the dead hashes DEAD in the record; stripping them from the docs is necessary and NOT sufficient — omission is invisible, a DEAD marker is not.** ⊕ The `SUPERSEDED-DO-NOT-FLASH-…` rename fixes the FILESYSTEM and does nothing for the TRANSCRIPT; both need doing. ⊕ Caught by the BUILDER, pushing back on the orchestrator's own filename which had called them a "dry run" — they were a real cut, and that distinction IS the hazard.
- 🛑🛑★★★★★ [The anti-damping is NOT the PID](accord-anti-damping-is-not-the-pid.md) — at 6–9 Hz P **−0.145** / I **−0.053** / D **+0.077** ⇒ **NET −0.121 = DAMPING**, while measured `Re(Z)` = **−3375 ct·s/rad = ANTI-DAMPING** at coherence 0.769 vs a shuffled 0.001 (221 windows / 884.5 s, 37× V89's exposure). ⇒ **it is another aggregator lane, or the PLANT — every remaining firmware candidate has to answer this.** ⊕ `Re(Z)` **flips sign at ~26 Hz** (anti-damped 2–26, damped 26–35) ⇒ **grind #2's band is not anti-damped at all.** 🛑 **The decisive experiment is the MANUAL HANDS-OFF COAST and the whole corpus has 2 windows / 21.4 s of it** — ~15–20 min of driving, and it yields 12–16 clean ring-down edges free. 🛑 Two opposite sign conventions are in play; confusing them inverts the result.
- 🛑🛑★★★★★ [Six levers CLOSED — five on ARITHMETIC, not on a null](accord-six-levers-closed-on-arithmetic.md) — **`0xCBE74`** (int32 wraparound at **1.6005×** ⇒ **×1.5 is 94 % of the lever's ENTIRE range**, and delivered damping is **5–69× below the resolvability floor**) · **`0xC63A6`** (micro-regime p50 **7.1 ct** = 0.22 % of the clamp, against a **PRE-REGISTERED ≲32 ct** line) · **the `Kd` cut** (D pumps only 2–12 Hz and **DAMPS 16–35**; costs **−0.217 at 18–22** and **−0.336 at 26–31** to buy +0.077) · **K1** (collinear with `|model|` above 1 °/s; discriminating cell **0.63 %** of engaged frames) · **term 0 / mixer lane 2** (severed by `0xC616C` = 0) · **`0xC520C`** as the return explanation (**misses by 8.3×**). 🛑 An arithmetic kill cannot be re-opened by more exposure or a bigger dose.
- 🛑🛑★★★★ [`0xC616C` = 0 — NEVER RAISE](accord-c616c-never-raise-driver-torque-relay.md) — virgin on every build and it **looks like a free untried lever**. It feeds `r14 = sign(driver torque) × cal` into `gp-0x6b76` → mixer lane 2 → `gp-0x6b4a` → **term 0 → the driver-feel reference `gp-0x6ad6`**. **Raising it is a Coulomb relay on DRIVER-TORQUE SIGN — the V80 class, arguably worse than `0xC4080`**, because driver-torque sign reverses on every micro-correction. ⊕ Its zero is also what makes **mixer lane 2 structurally inert today**, so a phase characterisation of that lane is moot. Three other gains in the same cascade are independently 0.0 ⇒ shipped-disabled (BELIEF, not traced).
- 🛑★★★★ [The rate-channel rule, and its SCOPE](accord-rate-channel-rule-and-its-scope.md) — **PHASE/IMPEDANCE ⇒ `rate_f` (`0x18F`)**: `tq` and `rate_f` share a frame so the skew **cancels exactly**; using `rate_c` **inverts the sign at 18–22 and 26–31 Hz at identical coherence** (−0.336 DAMPING becomes +0.184 PUMPING) — **the opposite build decision from the same data.** 🛑 **ABSOLUTE MAGNITUDE ⇒ `rate_c` (`0x14A`)**: measured slopes vs differentiated angle are `rate_f` **0.743–0.767 (reads ~24 % LOW)** vs `rate_c` **0.952–0.963**. ⇒ **STATE WHICH CHANNEL YOU USED, EVERY TIME.**
- 🛑★★★★ [A same-firmware PLACEBO PAIR is mandatory for any cross-build claim](accord-placebo-pair-is-mandatory-for-cross-build-claims.md) — route 77 ÷ route 75 is **BYTE-IDENTICAL FIRMWARE** and returned `e_6-9` **1.288 [1.017, 1.661]** with the **32–38 Hz band contrast ALSO excluding 1.00**; r77 ÷ r76 gave `e_18-22` **1.333 [1.001, 2.307]**. ⇒ **the band contrast does NOT rescue a thin cut.** Honest floors: **±16–22 % contrasted, ±33 % raw.** ⊕ A no-hypothesis placebo BAND (`e_10-16`) is the cheap tell for a stratum artefact. ⊕ The cheapest real fix is not statistical — **fly the next build on the SAME ROUTE.**
- 🛑★★★★ [`gp-0x6afe` ≡ `gp-0x6b4e` ≡ 0, ALWAYS, on every build](accord-gp6afe-gp6b4e-are-always-zero.md) — its source array `gp-0x62c8[0..10]` is written to literal **zero** or not written at all (**role 7 never appears in `0xC4124` on any build**), and the `.data` boot initialiser is **22 bytes, all zero** ⇒ the shaper's `iVar45 = gp-0x6afe + uVar34` reduces to `iVar45 = uVar34` ⇒ **there is NO second, independent LKAS injection at the final stage.** **CORRECTS the starred `accord-aggregator-reaches-motor-via-gp6acc-bridge` ("CAN/arbitration term") and `reference-accord-shaper-fun42af8` ("feed-forward addend"), and the team-lead's own brief.** ⚠ Its unity weight `0xC63A8` = 1024 is still correct — a unity weight on a zero lane is still zero.
- ★★★★ [`gp-0x6b4a` is a SECOND, direct LKAS term into `gp-0x6ad6`](accord-gp6b4a-is-a-second-direct-lkas-term.md) — term 0 of `FUN_00037fe6`: **unconditional, negated, NO calibration weight anywhere on its path** (no `mulh` executes on it, instruction-verified), and its gate window **±25600 equals the cell's own final clamp** ⇒ **it alone can drive the reference to its rail** (term 7 is capped at 32 %). **Structurally REINFORCING** — term 0's negation and `error = gp-0x4f60 − bias` cancel exactly. 🛑 **The golden model's `[VERIFIED]` block at `eps_lkas_chain_model.py:2318-2344` documented only the SIBLING `gp-0x6b4c`** ⇒ a `[VERIFIED]` tag certifies what was checked, not that nothing else is there. ⚠ `gp-0x67ab`'s trigger is still unresolved: if it is usually 1, term 0 is the ENTIRE story.
- ★★★★ [V90 flew — PROBE-ONLY, so it is the CONTROL CONDITION](accord-v90-flew-probe-only-control-condition.md) — route `77`, **17.91 engaged min / 86.41 %**, fault-free, **identity PASS `b4 == 0` on 124,362/124,362 frames** (impossible on V86B–V89). **Byte-identical to V89 in every cal cell**, so the operator's *"grind #1 still exists · micro-ratcheting still exists · grind #2 on highway-speed curves or lane changes"* is a control, **not a failed fix**. Deliverables: **`gp-0x6b26`'s full distribution with clamp duty EXACTLY 0.000000 in every stratum** (engaged p50 5.5 / p99 114.3 / max 319.1 vs ±511 ⇒ **not a relay today**) and **the observer gate `gp-0x6c00` measured for the first time — it NEVER fails, 0 of 124,362 frames.** 🛑 The clipping ladder (1.60×/2.75×/4.45×) is **NOT a dose budget.**
- 🛑🛑★★★★★ [The `0xCBE74` friction row has ZERO clean flights on a LIVE column](reference-accord-cbe74-friction-row-zero-clean-flights.md) — byte-verified by dereferencing `0xCBE74 + mode*4`: **V73 wrote mode 10 only (DISENGAGED) ⇒ its clean flight tests NOTHING**; ×1.5 on modes 24/26 flew **twice — V74 and V75 — and BOTH hard-faulted**; V76's flown artefact had it reverted. 🛑 **Inverts the standing attribution: V73 carried `0xC407E` = 850 and flew clean** ⇒ the row moves **exonerated → OPEN SUSPECT** (64 differing V73→V74 runs ⇒ cannot be pinned). ⚠ At MODE level it is 1-for-1, not 2-for-2: V74 faulted in MANUAL where m24 was byte-Honda; V75 faulted ENGAGED where m26 was ×1.5. ⚠ **Two artefacts share the V76 number — the BASE column is the discriminator, a glob is not a check.**
- 🛑★★★★ [AN ADDRESS IS NOT A MODE](reference-accord-an-address-is-not-a-mode.md) — three instances in one session (V69's mode-10 `gain_B`; "mode 24 dosed" read off a stock table; `0xD6A5C` named as mode 24 when it is **mode 23**). Byte-verified friction mode map m10/23/24/25/26/27 → `0xD2A44`/`0xD6A54`/`0xD6A64`/`0xD7A44`/`0xD7A54`/`0xD7A64`. 🛑 **Y is at record base + 8**; writing it at `base+2` lands in the X array, compared **UNSIGNED** (`−29490` → `36046`) ⇒ flat `Y[0]` at every speed = **a silent, plausible 5× highway increase that byte-verifies and drives.** Rule: dereference `0xCBE74 + mode*4` and print the mode beside every address in a spec.
- 🛑★★★★ [A COUNT is not a PHYSICAL FACT](feedback-a-count-is-not-a-physical-fact.md) — four instances: r76's index "drift" (bookkeeping, not timing — its payload age is the cleanest of three routes); the `gp-0x6752` writer census (a disp16 scan is blind to the 6-byte form ⇒ a lower bound, not a count); V86's rung map applied to V89 (**V86B swapped b5/b6 and V87–V89 inherit V86B**); and "payload age vs the newest `0x18F`" = 0.000 ms, which **assumes its own conclusion**. Rule: name the physical quantity, measure THAT, and ask what the metric assumes.
- 🛑★★★★ [Neither Ghidra nor Python alone can ENUMERATE](feedback-neither-ghidra-nor-python-alone-is-complete.md) — `search_instructions` returned **73 `ld.h` hits with `truncated:false` against 3 more real sites** in unanalysed regions; a naive Python scan misses the 6-byte form (`disp=(sext16(hw2)<<7)|((hw1>>4)&0x7F)`); and 🛑 **operand-text search cannot see register-indirect writes at all** — `clr1 0x1,0x6aec[r18]` behind `movhi -0x121,r0,r18` is a live writer of `gp-0x1514` whose text never contains `1514`. ⇒ **"0 writers" from a text search is not a null**, and GATE 1 rests on exactly that null. Both tools, set-differenced, every disagreement adjudicated.
- 🛑★★★★ [Three signal identities re-asserted wrongly in one session](reference-accord-three-signal-identity-recurrences.md) — `gp-0x6a5e` is **voted VEHICLE SPEED** (settled 2026-07-29; the earlier version already cost V44/V47) · `gp-0x4f50` is a **RATE** — the wrap-correction on a modular difference proves it, and `0x8000` on the stored angle is an **INIT/INVALID sentinel** · `gp-0x6afe` **≡** `gp-0x6b4e` (the mixer's tail-call `FUN_00042ac6` writes it ⇒ one probe rung, not two). 🛑 **All three were caught by a teammate cross-checking an INHERITED claim, not by the agent that made it, and two were weeks old** ⇒ an inherited signal label is a **BELIEF** until re-derived from the producer's arithmetic.
- ✅★★★★ [POLARITY VERIFIED: more modelled friction = MORE assist](accord-friction-polarity-more-friction-is-more-assist.md) — nine links, Ghidra-traced: friction subtracted from the model → residual ↓ → `gp-0x6b70` more negative → `gp-0x6ad6` (**a torque-tracking REFERENCE, not a motor torque**) ↓ → `error = measured driver torque − reference` ↑ → PID ↑ → aggregator (`mov`, `add`×8, **no negation**) ↑ ⇒ **LIGHTER wheel; V89 does NOT fight LKAS.** Coulomb confirmed 5 ways (`gp-0x6abc`←`gp-0x4f50` = motor rate; companion cal `0xC646E` is the kit's own "INERTIA gain"). 🛑 **V56 muted this lane and got a null — but scored on 15–26 Hz, NOT 6–9 Hz, and route 24 is not on disk.** Band-scoped, carried as general.
- ★★★★★ [V89 BUILT — the first build to touch the PLANT MODEL](accord-v89-built-the-plant-model-friction.md) — `0xC40D2` 102→204: K1, the `|model|`-proportional **modelled Coulomb friction** in `FUN_0003b8f6`, 2.000× (**1 reader / 0 writers, virgin on all 88 builds**), + a cave probe on `gp-0x6ae2` = friction×1024. The chain is a **DISTURBANCE OBSERVER** (`residual = MODEL − ACTUAL`) ⇒ under-modelled friction is chased = stick-slip. **8 bytes, zero unattributed, 50/50 CRC.** 🛑 `0xC4080` (NEVER-RAISE relay hazard) untouched. 🛑 Direction measured, **K1≡gate is BELIEF**. ⚠ may feel notchier on-centre.
- ★★★★★ [Engagement amplifies 6-9 Hz 2.8× via a COMMAND-PROPORTIONAL COULOMB RELAY](accord-engagement-amplifies-6-9hz-via-coulomb-relay.md) — full corpus, 30 routes / 284 min / 235 blocks. **`eng` band contrast +0.413 [+0.146, +0.667]** ⇒ engaging multiplies the ratchet band **2.8×**, 1.5× more than a control band, **and it does NOT grow with wheel rate** (+0.022 [−0.070, +0.116] ⇒ **REFUTES the earlier +0.144**). 🛑 **⇒ nothing argues for limiting the LKAS command's angle rate — the target is a GAIN.** 🛑🛑 **De-relaying it made things WORSE: `0xC40BC` 600 → 2.89× vs 6000 → 6.58× (contrast +0.682 [+0.213, +1.166]) ⇒ the standing "FREEZE at 6000" is CONTRADICTED; the car is at 600 and that is better.** ★ Column FRICTION damps this mode — grip −0.655 vs control −0.266, disjoint. 🛑 Loader bugs: per-segment caches were skipped; `_{tag}_*_plain_image.bin` misses `_v67_plain_image.bin`.
- ★★★★★ [The ratchet scales with WHEEL RATE — the operator's own axis](accord-ratchet-scales-with-wheel-rate.md) — engaged/manual 6–9 Hz **1.16× at 2 °/s → 3.94× [2.19, 6.70] at 100 °/s**; corr(log rate, log speed) = **−0.640** so D5's creep→highway AMPLITUDE decay is partly a rate effect (the FREQUENCY result stands). ★★ **The mode is strongly damped by DRIVER GRIP: −0.720 [−0.918, −0.500] vs control −0.216, CIs DISJOINT.** 🛑 Band-specificity of the rate term is **BELIEF** (contrast +0.144 [−0.004, +0.267]). 🛑 Two traps: per-band order vetoes build different window sets; `_cache_r66`/`r66x` are the SAME route and double-counting alone flipped the headline CI.
- 🛑🛑 [The base-assist damper CANNOT reach the micro regime](accord-base-assist-damper-cannot-reach-the-micro-regime.md) — `ch₀ = (FactorC(speed) × FactorE(rate)) >> 10` is a PRODUCT of two dead zones (35 km/h, 12.7 °/s); **zero on 95.91 % of engaged frames and 100 % of the micro regime**. Neither prior test ever had BOTH open (V86B armed FactorC only). 🛑 **But sizing kills it: 25 % authority at 10 °/s needs `FactorE Y[0]` off zero = a step at zero rate = the V80 "worst grinding ever" move.** FactorE `X[0]` is **60 counts**, not 12.
- 🛑 [The Lever-B × rate discriminator is UNDERPOWERED](accord-leverb-rate-discriminator-underpowered.md) — contrast −0.101 [−0.381, +0.298], **CI half-width 2.4× the effect** ⇒ **r24's engaged arm is NOT exonerated**. Closing it needs ~4× the episode blocks: matched ENGAGED *and* MANUAL exposure at matched WHEEL RATE. **The binding constraint on V89 is EXPOSURE, not analysis.**
# Memory Index — Honda EPS Firmware Project

- ★★★★★ [The ~7.79 Hz RATCHETING is a LINEAR LOOP OSCILLATION](accord-ratchet-is-a-linear-loop-oscillation.md) — measured **NOT a relay** (odd/even comb 0.858 [0.739, 1.000] vs a control firing at **1.204 on 15% injection**; PLV z ≤ 1.05; no 3rd harmonic ⇒ **<15% can be relay-generated**) and **NOT a plant resonance** (the wheel-on-bar mode is **12.8 Hz, ABOVE it**). ⇒ **a linear loop mode set by accumulated estimator lag** — fits speed-invariance (+0.074 Hz/m/s vs order-2's +0.961), engaged-only, in the bar but not the command. 🛑 **⇒ the lever class is PHASE/LAG, new since V38.** ⊕ NEW: a discrete engaged-only **~20.90 Hz** creep line on V85/V84, absent on V81 — **that** is the 18–22 Hz elevation, not a floor shift. ⚠ micro vs macro could **not** be separated.
- ★★★★ [V85 flew route 6e: the lever DELIVERED, the bands are a NULL](accord-v85-flew-lever-delivered-bands-are-null.md) — fault-free and the **cleanest flight in the lineage** (`STEER_STATUS` = {0: 43,641}). `0xC40BC` cut relay saturation **33.3% → 4.6% engaged (7.21×)**, both duty predictions hit. 🛑 **FREEZE at 6000 — and the "N is flat at 6000" reason was WRONG**: the ring rides on a bias 5–10× its amplitude ⇒ the **biased** DF is the instrument (pinning 0.0000 / 0.043 after 20.3×). 🛑 **Every band is a null** [0.63, 1.50]; **operator: ratcheting STILL UNFIXED.** 🛑 **EXPOSURE-LIMITED — 22.4 s ≥80 km/h ⇒ no highway/26–31 Hz verdict.** 🛑 `probe_build=['V80']` in `r6e.npz` is a **stale heuristic, WRONG**.
- ★★★★ [The plant-model → residual → assist-aggregator chain, end to end](accord-plant-model-residual-aggregator-chain.md) — `FUN_0003b8f6` → `FUN_0003bc20` → `FUN_00038148` (`gp-0x6b70`) → `FUN_00037fe6` (`gp-0x6ad6`) → PID → motor, with dual-encoding censuses. `0xC64AD..B3` are **0/1 ENABLE FLAGS, not gains** (`0xC64B0` gates `gp-0x6b70`); `0xC6200` is **NOT governor-shared** but **3 of its 15 readers are unidentified**. 🛑🛑 **THREE flatten-into-a-relay hazards: `0xC4080` NEVER RAISE · `0xC63AE` never →0 · `0xC6200` never < Y[0].** 🛑 `gp-0x67fa` ≡ **{11} alone** ⇒ **`0x454FE` is MEASURED INERT**; `gp-0x671a` **ruled out**. ⚠ the RAM LERP's `Y[0]` is `ep`-relative and **UNRESOLVED**.
- 🛑 [Eight levers killed on evidence, 2026-08-09](accord-levers-killed-2026-08-09.md) — `gain_A` **ENGAGED-INERT** (`0x3AB5E` overwrites it with 512; twice-failed pre-registered test) · **Lever A DO-NOT-RESTORE** (ungated ⇒ V62/V65 manual verbatim; ⚠ its int16-overflow leg **WITHDRAWN**) · the 13-point LERP **DEAD** (`gp-0x6a10` is **absolute steering angle**, 88.6% in the flat segment) · **FactorD structurally inert** (FactorC's `X[0]`=34.97 km/h, `Y[0]`=0, ×3 confirmations) · `0xC63A0`→2048 **INERT** (`ch₀`=0 on 98.8% of engaged frames) · `0xC61F6`→0 **DO NOT** (deadband is the relay's dual) · `0xC61D6` **already rejected**. 🛑 **⇒ THIS FIRMWARE HAS NO FREQUENCY-SELECTIVE LEVER.**
- ★★★★ [V86 BUILT, UNFLASHED — the kit's first PHASE/LAG lever](accord-v86-built-the-frequency-lever.md) — one cell `0xC40D4` **573 → 286** (command-branch EMA, α 0.1399 → 0.0698). Pre-registered as a **FREQUENCY ratio `f(V86)/f(V85) ∈ [0.797, 0.875]`** = 3.3 FFT bins; **FALSIFIED if it stays at 7.79 Hz**; **AMBIGUOUS is a declared outcome, not a null**. 🛑 **An EMA CANNOT limit max angle rate — `|H(0)| = 1` exactly for every α** (verified to 1.000000000000). Mode-proof (573 unique in `[0xC4000,0xC4200)`, no repeating stride). Probe `b7⇒b6`/`b5⇒b6` are exact **duals** of V85's ⇒ identity is free. ⚠ **V86B ON HOLD** — settled by a zero-byte creep protocol, not a build.
- 🛑 [A falsifier only fires if it COULD have fired](feedback-a-falsifier-only-fires-if-it-could-have-fired.md) — V84's `b7`/`b6` tested `|r24| ≥ 1024` on a lane capped at `|r1| = 201` ⇒ 0.0 in **both** arms, misread as "out of force"; and V85's damper abort "passed" on **22.4 s** of exposure. **RULE 5 applied to the INSTRUMENT** — the kit has now made the error at both ends.
- 🛑 [A count-only record census is blind to a write into an already-non-stock record](feedback-record-census-must-assert-every-record.md) — assert **every** record byte-identical to the **BASE** unless declared. It is **340 pointer slots (10 arrays × 34 modes), not 58**; **34 non-stock records**, including modes **32/33**.
- 🛑 [Smoke-test GhidraMCP at agent spawn — `check_tools` lies](feedback-smoke-test-ghidra-tools-at-agent-spawn.md) — registration is scoped to the session at spawn time; three tracers were **silently blind all session** while `check_tools` said "callable". **First call must be `get_current_program_info`.** A blind tracer's output is shaped exactly like a genuine null.
- 🛑 [A right answer for the wrong reason is a coincidence](feedback-right-answer-wrong-reason-is-a-coincidence.md) — V85's *"N is flat at 6000"* reached the right cell and the **wrong action** (revert, not freeze). Confirmation of the outcome is not confirmation of the model; record the corrected mechanism in place even when the recommendation is unchanged.
- ★★★★★ [V84 flew as route 6d and FIXED the highway ring](accord-v84-flew-and-fixed-the-highway-ring.md) — 26–31 Hz burst duty **25.1% → 2.54% on 3.4× the exposure**, the kit's second measured fix. 🛑 **RETRACTS V83a's "damper-dose model of the ring is FALSIFIED"** — V83a never removed the damper (mode 27 kept it) and had 19.2 s of highway. ⊕ **V84 is byte-identical to V67/V68 at every grind-relevant cell** ⇒ the rate lane is maxed out at V67's level. S2/S3/S4 all FAIL.
- ★★★★★ [FUN_0003b8f6 is a Coulomb relay proportional to the COMMAND](accord-fun3b8f6-coulomb-relay-proportional-to-command.md) — 1 kHz, undocumented until 2026-08-09. `ratio` saturates at 50 counts against a 13000 gate ⇒ pinned across **99.62%** of its range; **relay index 7.87 vs V80's 3.27**. Lever `0xC40BC`, **1 reader / 0 writers, virgin on all 84 builds**. 🛑 V56's full-lane mute bounds it: **do not expect 18–22 Hz to move.**
- ★★★★★ [The loop does NOT close through openpilot](accord-loop-does-not-close-through-openpilot.md) — the bar **leads** the command by **18.5 ms**; three methods agree with a working 8× positive control. **91–98% of bar band power is INCOHERENT with the command** ⇒ command-side filtering has nothing to remove. 🛑 Also: the *"`0x18F` is one frame stale"* rule is a **cache-extractor artefact, not a bus property**.
- ★★★★★ [The firmware ADDS torque to the bar, engaged, at low speed](accord-firmware-adds-torque-to-bar-engaged-at-low-speed.md) — at matched `θ̈` and matched speed, engaged/manual = **2.77× (6–9 Hz)**, **1.66× (17–23 Hz)**, control **1.04**. Purely inertial coupling would give 1.00. ⚠ The control FAILS above 8 m/s, so only the 2–8 m/s row is clean. ⊕ Wheel-on-bar mode is **12.8 Hz — between the two symptoms** ⇒ a single-gain `θ̈` feedforward arrives at S1 **inverted**.
- ★★★★★ [LKAS authority has been FROZEN since V38](accord-lkas-authority-frozen-since-v38.md) — gain 3564 / setpoint 16384 / clamps 2048 **byte-identical on all 53 images V38→V84**, ~30 flights. The excitation variable has never been an arm. ⊕ But **command buys DUTY (5–45×), not AMPLITUDE (≤2.3×)** — the standing rule survives. 🛑 `0x454FE` **cannot execute** (state 4 fires 0/123,277) and V42 was never single-lever.
- [Run as a pure orchestrator; verify at the end](feedback-orchestrator-mode-delegate-verify-at-end.md) — operator wants the lead to delegate all digging to subagents and only open Ghidra/fine-details to finalize the picture or resolve a subagent dispute.
- [Damper variant ROW vs INDEX trap](reference-accord-damper-variant-row-vs-index-trap.md) — 3-stage indirection (EEPROM ID → ROW via 16-key match → INDEX via a second table → ptr array); this car's PN → row 2 → index 10 → the SAME tables V44/V47 edited, so their nulls likely are real.
- [Double-check subagent conclusions; never relay unverified](feedback-verify-subagent-conclusions.md) — in orchestrator mode, run a minimal independent confirmation of any decision-bearing subagent claim (tight script / paper decode / math check), even in the safe direction. Operator emphasized twice (2026-07-23/24); extends the older [[feedback_verify_subagent_claims]].
- [Iron rule: full-frame logging on every live CAN op](feedback_full_frame_logging.md) — log every TX + relevant RX (not summaries); working transport = Panda(disable_checks=True)+set_safety_mode(3) ELM327 bus 1 (ALLOUTPUT/set_obd don't reach the EPS)

- [⚠ CORRECTION OF RECORD: gp-0x4f60 is SENSOR-B DRIVER COLUMN TORQUE, not angular velocity / not vehicle speed](reference_accord_gp4f60_is_sensor_b_column_torque.md) — proven by CAN-399 packer FUN_00055c42 (STEER_TORQUE_SENSOR = -(gp-0x4f60 × 125/128)). - [Accord LKAS setpoint ±15360 clamp = a DEGENERATE (flat) LERP; raising it is safe but needs a new CRC block](reference_accord_setpoint_limit_15360_lerp.md) — all 28 records across 5 banks have a FLAT 15360 Y row, so the axis (gp-0x6a5e = **vehicle speed**, corrected 2026-08-06, was mislabeled driver torque — a latent tuning surface shipped flattened) is irrelevant; 0xCB844 has exactly ONE code reader (0x28FCE); A160 (row 11 TVCA4) → gp-0x674e=**7** (corrected 2026-08-06, was wrongly assumed 1 from a stale row-2/TVAA1 premise) → live record **0xE51A8**, Y row 0xE51BC..0xE51CC (9 halfwords). openpilot Accord torqueBP=4096 → setpoint 16384 > 15360, so top 6.25% of range is clipped; raise → **+6.71% top-end at EVERY build tier**. EME-safe (no float 15360.0 image-wide; lockstep casts the same int gp-0x6acc @0x4467a; gentle-EME channel is driver-fed). ✅ **BUILD BLOCKER RESOLVED + SHIPPED IN V38 (2026-07-18)**: added `(0xE4000,0xE4FFC)`+`(0xE5000,0xE5FFC)` to `TOUCHED_BLOCKS` — first build in this kit to touch a bootloader block outside the compact `0xC6xxx` cal block; the 49-block chain survives because the linked-list page fields at `block_start-8/-6` live in the **preceding** block, so only the trailers move. V38 patches **all 8 selector-reachable records** (sel {0,1,3,4,6,7,8,9}) including the true live sel-7 `0xE51A8`, so the raise stands regardless of the slot-provenance caveat

- [★ FUN_00043e44 watchdog fault SM: 7 weighted flags, threshold 128, ~10-cycle debounce → DTC setter](reference_accord_watchdog_fault_sm_fun43e44.md) — 7 plausibility flags weights 1/2/4/8/16/32/64 (max sum **127**) vs threshold **128.0** — unreachable in one cycle BY DESIGN; the trip comes from a debounce SM (gp-0x3540/gp-0x3550, integrate 0.001/cyc to 0.01 ≈ **10 cycles ≈ 0.1s**, decay 0.0005) that adds **1024.0** → 1151 > 128. Trip → `FUN_000462e6(0x3f1b)` → **`FUN_00016de6(0x1D, 0x3f1b, 1, 1)`**. **RESOLVES a memory contradiction — `corridor_lockstep` RIGHT, `override_snap` WRONG (retracted in place)**: TWO monitors — M1 `FUN_00042af8`/`gp-0x3564` (int +10/cyc thr100) → `FUN_00016de6(0x1c,…)`, M2 `FUN_00043e44`/`gp-0x3550` (float +0.001/cyc thr0.01) → `FUN_00016de6(0x1d,…)`. **`gp-0x3564` traces to MOTOR-OFF** (`FUN_0001611e` bits0x41 → `FUN_00018738` → gp-0x685c=1 → gp-0x3ef8=1 → `FUN_00019f7c` → gp-0x67fa=8 → `FUN_00045608(3,0,0x8000,0x8000)` → gp-0x3ee8=1, **power-cycle to recover**) = the V25/V26 brick path. ⚠ **An agent-memory "Monitor 2 PERMANENTLY GATED OFF" verdict was an off-by-0x1000 misread**: gate is `tp+0x74a4` = **0xC64A4 = 0x00 = ENABLED** (trip REACHABLE), not 0xC74A4=0xEA. **These monitors are hard-shutdown capable, NOT advisory.** Whether index 0x1d is hard-fault-eligible (bits 0x41) is UNVERIFIED. ✅ **Closes the V38 setpoint-slew [OPEN]**: `FUN_00043e44` never reads the setpoint or the gain — it consumes gp-0x6acc as an INPUT and **every tolerance compares its own float re-derivation vs the int shaper's stored result**, so a clamp raise translates BOTH sides; plus the per-cycle slew is bounded upstream on gp-0x6ace by cals 0xC6206=512/0xC6208=205, independent of the clamp. ⚠ **POLARITY TRAP**: the ±8.0 compare on gp-0x6acc is a **sanitize-to-zero (out-of-range→0)**, NOT a gate excluding the command — an earlier draft had it backwards; check against the ±25.0 gp-0x4f60 reference @0x43eda. ⚠ Also: cal **0xC64DE is NOT a command ramp step** (all 18 reads in the arbitration/STEER_STATUS region) — the V18 "EME ramp" label is unsupported

- [★ Accord CAN-TX architecture + how to add a NEW TX ID](reference-accord-can-tx-architecture-new-id.md) — single FCN0; 18 slots via 0xB7208(route)/0xB721C(ID=entry>>18)/cadence 0xB7C9C; ALL broadcast IDs ride mailbox 6; FREE mailbox pool 7-32 (recommend 16); new ID = dedicated free mailbox + small cave. ★ Gateway verdict (lead-verified): the visible/absent split is a DOWNSTREAM gateway per-ID whitelist (the 4 absent IDs are actively fired at real rates, incl. 0x19F at 62.5 Hz = same as visible 399, yet dropped) → a new ID won't reach the comma's built-in panda; new-ID telemetry needs a red-panda tap directly on the EPS bus. Resolves the old can330-tx-rate + 2026-07-07 car-facing questions.
- [★ Accord CAN broadcast visibility = EXTERNAL gateway (single FCN0)](reference_accord_can_single_fcn0_external_gateway.md) — 2026-07-08 PROVEN: one controller FCN0/mailbox-6 for ALL frames (FCN1 never init'd, zero xrefs @0xFF4A0000); 0x660 & 399 leave on the SAME wire (routing tbl 0xB7208 all=6; emitter FUN_0001d68e; slot→ID 0xB721C) → the 399/427/0x14A-visible vs 0x660/0x19F/0x32E/0x64D-absent split is 100% the car gateway, NOT the EPS. DID 0xF181=idx25 (part string the flasher reads — DON'T touch). SID 0x23 ABSENT → PATCH required: repurpose/add a 0xB7800 DID → cave 0xC4E00 handler reusing RAM-read primitive FUN_0001CA0A (no SA); ABI = set *(u16)(ctx+0xc)=data_len+2; FUN_000211ba(); FUN_0002114e(abs_ptr,n) append; FUN_0002073a(). - [Accord sub-3mph LKAS cutoff is OPENPILOT-side, NOT a firmware pipeline gate](reference_accord_sub3mph_lkas_openpilot_gate.md) — 2026-07-17: a firmware trace found NO speed threshold in CAN-decode→arbitration→decider→engage-SM→mixer→shaper. 3 mph = openpilot STEER_GLOBAL_MIN_SPEED (3*MPH_TO_MS) AND Accord minSteerSpeed (3*MPH_TO_MS, values.py) → below max(those) controls runs latActive=False → STEER_REQUEST=0 (openpilot stops commanding). EPS also reports STEER_STATUS=LOW_SPEED_LOCKOUT near standstill (opendbc: "All Honda EPS cut off slightly above standstill"). ## 2026-07-21 — 🛑 V48B FLASHED → CATASTROPHIC (root-caused; two mandatory cave gates added)

Kit's THIRD cave brick (V24/V27/V48B). Root cause: (1) RAM COLLISION — biquad x2 cell `gp-0x14FA` high byte `0xFEDF6B07` aliases a live monitor/DTC status bitfield (readers `FUN_00051fbc`/`FUN_00053f32` case 8), b2≈0.97 near-unity → external write injects full-scale → clamp ±25600 → motor slams; cave also stomps the monitor byte 1000×/s; writer unlocated but aliasing confirmed; `gp-0x14FA` in poison region `gp-0x1401..0x1502`, safe alt `gp-0x14E0`. (2) lightly-damped resonator (r=0.979, Q≈3.2) dropped into the always-on base-assist loop (no LKAS/speed gate) with NO closed-loop stability check — only `|N(21.4)|` inserted into the LKAS loop-gain model. (3) clock rate EXONERATED (undivided 1 kHz, correct). Handoff `docs/HANDOFF-2026-07-21-v48b-flashed-catastrophic.md`.
- [feedback-cave-two-gates-ram-ownership-and-closed-loop](feedback-cave-two-gates-ram-ownership-and-closed-loop.md) — ★ HARD RULE (apply without being asked): before ANY code cave / filter / dynamics change is flash-ready it MUST pass GATE 1 (RAM ownership — every byte's full footprint proven free incl. writers + register-indirect/6-byte-disp; `gp-0x1401..0x1502` poison) AND GATE 2 (closed-loop stability — magnitude+phase/Nyquist of EVERY loop the signal is in, esp. the always-on base-assist loop, element inserted; not a single-frequency magnitude). Byte/CRC/disassembly/open-loop-DSP verification is necessary but NOT sufficient — V48B passed all of them and bricked.

- [accord-dtc-0x18-hard-eligible-cadence-watchdog](../../../.claude/projects/C--Users-dudei-Desktop-Projects-accord-eps-torque-mod/memory/accord-dtc-0x18-hard-eligible-cadence-watchdog.md) *(user-store)* — 🛑 **CORRECTED 2026-08-06 — WRONG AS WRITTEN.** ~~DTC 0x18 is the per-task cadence/overrun watchdog, so ANY cave on the 1 kHz path has a timing budget.~~ `0x18` is a **BOOT-TIME RESET-CAUSE REPORT, not a live per-task deadline monitor**: `FUN_00014b3e` (the 4-task liveness checker) **never calls the DTC latch chain** — it snapshots the faulting PC, writes an NVM cause-code and reboots; the real `FUN_00016de6(0x18,…)` lives in the sibling `FUN_00014ba0`, gated on **reading that NVM cause-code back**, reached only from **state 1** of the dispatcher (a boot pass) ⇒ **it cannot be tripped by a running task.** The record value (0xB7FDC = 0x3D01) and the 2-byte-slip warning still stand. ⊕ **The V75 probe cave is EXONERATED**: 45→68 B costs **+17 cycles ≈ 212 ns** (V74 18 cy → V75 35 cy at 80 MHz) — **4–5 orders of magnitude below any window.**

- ★★ [reference-accord-fourframe-strb-ssam-defect](reference-accord-fourframe-strb-ssam-defect.md) — **FOURFRAME NEVER TRANSMITTED, and it was OUR firmware.** The cave wrote `STRB = 0x80` to mailboxes 16-19; bit0 is `SSAM` and the V850E2 manual is explicit (`0 = Message buffer not used`), with §20.8.1 making SSAM=1 a hard precondition for joining the TX priority search. Stock `FUN_0001cf30` writes **0x01** to all seven TX mailboxes and **0x00** to the unused pool — `0x80` left ours configured exactly like the unused pool. Root cause: `build_vcantx_test_tva.py:54` mislabelled bit7 `SSOW` as "TX direction"; **the VCANTX seed carries the same defect.** Fix = `203e8000`→`203e0100` at 4 sites, 8 bytes, identical encoding. ⇒ **The gateway-whitelist theory is WEAKENED**: `0x19F` is gated at its own request site (`0x5559E`) while `0x18F` is not, so it was never a clean control.
- ★ [reference-accord-c646c-shared-gain-not-lkas-only](reference-accord-c646c-shared-gain-not-lkas-only.md) — **`0xC646C` is NOT "the LKAS authority gain"** — it is the firmware's single shared Q15 sensor-to-command scale with **exactly 6 readers** across three subsystems (enumerated twice, both encodings, no float mirror, neither monitor among them). Two of them (`0x36686`, `0x3684a`) apply it to the **raw torsion bar `gp-0x4f60`** on a feedback path verified end-to-end into `gp-0x6b94` → governor → motor. So 4× LKAS authority silently quadrupled two feedback lanes. **Probably NOT the 21 Hz driver** (that lane is IIR'd at fc≈2.2 Hz = −19.7 dB, clamped to 5% of aggregator range) and **the saturation hypothesis is empirically DEAD** (0 of 10,178 active-LKAS frames reach the clamp). Minimal fix designed + verified: one 2-byte retarget of `0x2a1ee` to a confirmed-free cal word at `0xC6CD0`.
- ★★ [reference-accord-vibration-needs-applied-torque](reference-accord-vibration-needs-applied-torque.md) — **the vibration needs the EPS to ACTUALLY APPLY LKAS torque, not merely to be commanded.** Three-way split on route 13: openpilot off → nothing; commanding *harder* but EPS in lockout → nothing (1.33× = noise); commanding **and applying** → **14,750×** more 21 Hz power. **Also: the 21 Hz IS in the openpilot command** — the "not in the output" premise was FALSE and came from reading `0xE4` on src=2/src=128, which are **identically zero**; only src=129 carries data. Coherence 0.685 @21 Hz vs 0.171 @1-3 Hz; **no OP-side low-pass at 21 Hz** (−2.70 dB) ⇒ **an OP notch is UNTESTED, not a no-op.** Retracts the "latency makes 21 Hz impossible" argument as bad control theory. Aliasing 21 vs 78.91 Hz still OPEN (IMU tie at the noise floor; leans 21.09 on implied Q).

That signal is what the EPS **transmits** as `STEER_ANGLE_RATE` (0x14A[2:3] and the 10× finer 0x18F[2:3]) ⇒ **opendbc ground truth**, and it carries the mode at **996×** vs torque's 877×. ⇒ the rate path **bypasses the torque EMA**, so the −1.29/−14.91 dB figures and the unresolved `FUN_00022ca0` task rate **matter far less than they appeared**. 🛑🛑 **GATE 2 ANSWERED *AGAINST* CUTTING IT — THE LEVER INVERTS.** The torque EMA is a **multiplicative amplitude scale**, not an additive branch, and the core term is `rate_error = baseline − angle_rate` (`sub r6,r28` @`0x34e96`) ⇒ **`gp-0x6bbe` ≈ −(gain)·angle_rate = viscous DAMPING.** **Cutting it would REMOVE damping and likely make the grinding WORSE — the V56 error one build later.** The interesting direction is **RAISING** the gain: cleanest lever **`K1` @`0xD200C`=43** (1 hit image-wide). ⚠ **INFERRED, not simulated** — rests on `baseline` being slow at 22 Hz; **certify by simulation before building.** Also: **speedLERP2 `0xD20C0` is FLAT** (fixed ±512 clamp), speedLERP1 is a hump peaking at 40 km/h, not a monotonic speed rise. **First candidate ever in the angle-rate domain** — all ~50 falsified levers were torque-domain.

Boost+damping run at 100 Hz ⇒ 37.6-75.2° ZOH lag at 20.9 Hz ⇒ a structural reason V44/V47 were null, independent of the FactorC speed axis. Also invalidates V59's eps table (bracketed 1 kHz/500 Hz).
- ★★★ [V61 FLASHED → WORSE: the rate lane is the DAMPER, not the amplifier](accord-rate-lane-is-the-damper-not-the-amplifier.md) — the kit's first SIGNED on-car result. r24/r26 = +Kd·d(T_bar)/dt added IN PHASE with assist (polarity is one shared load, combine chain is ten `add`s, no `sub`) ⇒ viscous damping across the torsion bar; at Kd=0 the mode has no damping term at all. Grinding worsened engaged AND appeared in manual/reverse. A derivative is DC-neutral, so "V61 removed assist" is ruled out. 🛑 Falsifies the golden model's "r26 = excitation-to-amplifier" framing; V39/V42/V61 all tested the lane DOWNWARD and bracket the wrong side of the optimum.
- ★★ [V62 BUILT: double the rate lane via two `sar` immediates](accord-v62-doubles-the-rate-lane.md) — 0x3AC20/0x3AB76 `sar 0xa`→`sar 0x9`, 6 bytes off V59, CAL CRC unchanged. Chosen over the gain cals because the arm is a priority chain that can't be pinned statically, r24's default arm is MODE-INDEXED via gp+0x63fd, and gp-0x683c is a dead gate. 🛑 0xD6AEC is mode 22, NOT a redundancy twin — the V27-desync reading was wrong. 🛑 0xC6C42 lockstep objection RETRACTED (single reader, broadcast in sync); D is a real V63 PHASE lever, 4→2 halves the lead's lag 15.1°→7.6°.
- 🛑 [A strict band presence-tests, it cannot LOCATE a mode that moved](accord-rate-lane-is-the-damper-not-the-amplifier.md#methodology-trap) — V61's first pass pre-restricted to 18-26 Hz and the argmax pinned to the band edge (18.04 Hz, sd 0.00) because the mode had shifted BELOW it. Locate over 12-30 Hz, then interpret. The mode moved 21.18 -> 18.25 Hz, which a pure gain change cannot do — that frequency shift is the structural proof the lane is a PHASE (lead) term.
- ★★★ [gp-0x671a is an OSCILLATION DETECTOR (hard-reversal counter)](accord-state671a-is-an-oscillation-detector.md) — FUN_000428d4 resets the count to 0 EVERY TICK while neutral and only leaves neutral if |gp-0x6c2c| > 12800; a reversal increments it, 50 quiet ticks clear it.
