- ★★★ [gp-0x671a is a ONE-WAY LATCH with a 5 s hold — V63/V64's decoupling is narrower than first claimed](accord-state671a-is-an-oscillation-detector.md) — FUN_000428d4's output stage re-pins the counter to CEIL once reached; the only way down is 5000 consecutive ticks with driver torque ≥ 640 (cal 0xC62DE) AND no reversals, and torque dips below 640 on every direction change. ⇒ "V62, but only after an oscillation has happened", NOT "only while oscillating". 🛑 The "zero manual-feel cost by construction" claim is WITHDRAWN. ✅ But the latch is PROTECTIVE — a per-tick-gated gain would modulate AT the mode frequency, i.e. a parametric pump.
- ★★ [V64 BUILT: V63 + the probe repointed at the detector](accord-v63-oscillation-gated-rate-damping.md) — operator's proposal. The old probe measured gp-0x6ba6, the pump V60 falsified, so a V63 null would have been uninterpretable. 0x14A byte4: bit6 = state>=5, bit5 = state!=0, bit4 = FSM left neutral, bit3 = r24 override. Actionable in EVERY failure mode (bit4 clear ⇒ lower T 0xC620A; bit4 set + bit6 clear ⇒ lower CEIL 0xC64FA). 68/68 cave bytes, zero budget left. ⚠ V850 ld.bu carries disp bit0 in hw1 bit5, not hw2 — a naive decode reports false mismatches.
- ★★★ [V64 FLASHED: the null is on the GATE, not the hypothesis](accord-state671a-is-an-oscillation-detector.md) — probe read a constant 0x87 across all 14,980 frames: gp-0x671a/gp-0x67df/gp-0x671d ZERO on every frame through 1,158 steering-rate reversals, so |gp-0x6c2c| never crossed T=12800 and 0xC6440/0xC643E were never in force. Spectra confirm V64 ≡ V59 (2-3 m/s bin: 20.98 vs 20.99 Hz, env99 1811 vs 1804). 🛑 Do NOT cite V64 as evidence against raising the rate lane. ⇒ V62 promoted to the recommended next flash. ⚠ Even if armed it delivers r24 x1.78 / r26 x1.00 — Honda's oscillation arms are gain REDUCTIONS (defaults 2305 and 3072 vs arms 2048 and 1536).
- ★★ [gp-0x6c2c is a MOTOR-RATE DERIVATIVE, not torque](accord-gp6c2c-is-the-detector-input.md) — the detector's only input; FUN_00041464 @0x4184E differences a smoothed gp-0x4f50, x32, twice EMA'd, >>9. Tripping T=12800 needs ~1683 counts at 21.3 Hz (frequency-domain cross-check: 1685), inside the ±13000 validity ceiling ⇒ the drive was ~1.7-2x short, NOT structurally blind. 🛑 Three bus-torque-derived numbers are VOID (T≈2048-2560 band, the 3.29x LSB bound, "per-tick ⇒ dead") — the bus bounds amplitude but not scale, and this firmware rescales by 8.000x between two copies of one signal.
- 🛑 [gp-0x671a has FOUR external consumers — T is not a free lever](accord-state671a-is-an-oscillation-detector.md) — FUN_0003a382 uses it as a CONTINUOUS LERP index into the live P/I/D lane gp-0x6ad4, FUN_00036c12's friction-comp sums into the SAME aggregator, plus FUN_000352b4/FUN_00035b20. Lowering T changes FIVE things at once. gp-0x67df by contrast is clean (2 hits, both internal). 🛑 0xC64FA (CEIL) is a BYTE cal = 5 — a halfword read gives 517 and is wrong.
- 🛑 [Probe the gate and the input, not just the output](feedback-probe-the-gate-not-just-the-output.md) — V64 removed V63's ambiguity and created a new one a layer up (did T never trip, or did the detector never run?), closable only by a post-hoc trace. Every gated mechanism is enable → input → threshold → state → effect; a probe on the state collapses everything upstream into one null. Ask "if every bit reads zero, how many stories explain that?"
- ★★★ [V62 FLASHED: THE GRINDING IS FIXED](accord-v62-flashed-grinding-is-fixed.md) — route 37, 86,278 frames. 18-22 Hz creep **0.124 [0.036, 0.387]** vs V59 (8x), **0.024 [0.016, 0.234] at |rate| 16-32 deg/s (42x)**, with a 30-40 Hz negative control at ~1.0 ⇒ band-specific. Transient rates 0.793/0.486/0.338 at >200/>500/>1000 — monotonically cleaner; lowest p90/p99/>1000-rate of ANY build. **The kit's first measured fix.** 🛑 The reported "new grinding" is NOT an established regression: the 43 excursions >2000 are ONE 0.92 s burst (n=1), V62's burst-rate CI **[0.00004, 0.00793] sits inside V59's [0, 0.00986]**, and an exposure-matched test gives **p = 0.51**. Instant #2 is an ordinary burst V59 produces 3x MORE often. ⇒ **No new build — fly V62 again and count bursts.** 🛑 **TWO CORRECTIONS 2026-08-04:** (a) the companion *"r26 is structurally inert ⇒ `0x3AB76` was a no-op"* claim is **REVERSED** — V62 doubled **both** lanes; the measured results stand, the single-lane attribution does not. (b) **V62's dose is near the OPTIMUM, not on a ramp** — V69's 4× brought grind #1 back (median `e_18-22` engaged creep 2501/879/**168**/109/746 at 0×/1×/2×/2×-gated/4×) ⇒ **do not read 8–42× as "more would be better."**
- 🛑 [The "r26 is INERT" claim SPLITS — one leg reversed, one downgraded](accord-r26-is-structurally-inert.md) — **filename kept so old links resolve. NOT a flat reversal** (that would be the mirror image of the original error). **LEG 1 — THE GATE: REVERSED [EVIDENCE].** `r26 == 0 ⟺ gp-0x6b5e != 0`, `gp-0x6b5e = ((LERP(gp-0x6bda) × 0xC63C2) >> 10) × polarity` (producer `FUN_000361c8` @`0x36256`/`0x36264`), trapezoid `0xC66CC` X = [−384,−128,128,294,384] Y = [0,4762,4762,717,0] ⇒ killed **only** at `|gp-0x6bda| ≥ 384` — and `gp-0x6bda` is a **margin to a peak-hold envelope of driver assist torque `gp-0x6bf0`** (`FUN_00036022` @`0x36068`–`0x3608C`; envelope half-width never < 9390), **≈ 9262 = 24× the threshold hands-off**. ⇒ **the gate does NOT kill r26 in ordinary driving**, least of all hands-off at creep; the kill window is a **~512-count sliver at the driver-override end** (cf. `0xC6156` = 9216). **LEG 2 — THE MAGNITUDE: STILL BELIEF, unresolved either way.** `0xC6564` really is 40 zero bytes with no writer found for the RAM adjustment (10 of 18 cells) ⇒ `stage1 ≈ 0` **IF** that cal base feeds `gp-0x69a4` — **but that link was NEVER VERIFIED**, and `gp-0x69a4`'s real producer is a **live runtime 10-segment LERP at `0x355C6` in `FUN_000352b4`** (1 writer / 3 readers: `0x355A4`, `0x3575A`, `0x3AB3A` = the aggregator). ⇒ **"r24 carries the entire lane" rests on LEG 2 alone, and the V42/V61/V62 re-attribution is CONTINGENT on it.** ★ **The one indirect argument that LEG 2 holds**: at `a ≈ 1` V67/V68's gate (gain_A 3072 → 512, a 6.00× cut) would put their engaged **total at ~0.94× stock** — essentially on stock — **yet V67/V68 measured the best grind #1 result in the kit (109 vs stock's 879)** ⇒ the empirical record argues **`a` is small**. ✅ **DIRECTLY MEASURABLE**: r24/r26 share **one** polarity load (`ld.b -0x6752[gp],r14` @`0x3AB78`, reused @`0x3AB7E`/`0x3AC3E`) so they **always carry the same sign** ⇒ `sign(gp-0x6adc)` vs `sign(gp-0x6ada)` is a matched pair — **bit4 pinned at 1 while bit3 toggles ⇒ r26 is zero; bit4 tracking bit3 ⇒ r26 is live.** V70 flies exactly that pair. ⚠ **`0xC6444` raising is genuinely UNTESTED** (V42 tested it **downward**, 512 → 0, falsified — the same "downward ≠ upward" distinction V61→V62 turned on); 1 reader / 0 writers, no float mirror, CRC block #48, ceiling ≤ 6553. 🛑 **A CANDIDATE, NOT A RECOMMENDATION — V70 does not take it**, because `a` is unmeasured and V67/V68's control path is the measured best. ✅ V62/V65's `sar` route is the **only** edit here that is dose-exact independent of `a`.
- ★★ [The ratchet is a SATURATED RESONANCE, not friction](accord-ratchet-is-a-saturated-resonance.md) — LKAS-gated at **Fisher OR 115.5, p = 1.09e-08** on episodes pooled across 5 builds (75.3% vs 2.1% presence); **waveform SYMMETRIC on every build** (skew(dx/dt) −0.16…+0.06 vs a −3.27 sawtooth calibration) ⇒ damping/loop gain, not stick-slip. Fixed in hertz (CV 0.211 vs order 0.829). ❌ Damper `gp-0x6bd0` **inert at both operating points** (f5 = 0 below 12.7 deg/s; ratchet median 2.0 deg/s) — a THIRD reason V44/V47 were null. ❌ Relay, friction-comp, and the motor-rate LERP as a discriminator all eliminated. ✅ Open: gain_B's X **breakpoints** are calibration and the modes DO separate on rate (9.4 vs 73.0 counts).
- 🛑 [Bootstrap over EPISODES, and measure the noise floor first](feedback-episodes-not-windows-and-the-noise-floor.md) — windows within a run are correlated; a window bootstrap shrinks CIs by ~√28 and manufactures significance. Split-half-by-episode null gave a **2.2-2.8x resolution floor**; judge ratios against that, not 1.0. Retracted three of the orchestrator's own claims in one session, incl. a Simpson's-paradox f0 "shift" and an effort-gating that was really **motor rate** (partial ρ(effort|rate) NEGATIVE, ρ(rate|effort) +0.57…+0.70). ★ Prefer `|d(tq)|` for cross-route work (null 0.87-0.92 vs 0.63-1.23 for band power).
- 🛑 [0x14A byte4 = 0x87 means the OPPOSITE thing on V62 vs V64](accord-v62-flashed-grinding-is-fixed.md) — V59's thermometer (which V62 carries) reads it as "boost index ≥ 2048", the DEEPEST reading; V64's detector reads it as "unarmed", the V64 null. It is 9.24% of route 37 and the V64 handoff primes everyone to see a null. Always confirm which probe the flown build carries before decoding byte4.
- 🛑 [Byte scans confirm instructions that DO NOT EXIST — 5 false positives in one afternoon](accord-formatv-aliasing-five-false-positives.md) — building V65's cave, a raw LE scan "found" `cmp -0x2,rN` at four addresses and a `bgt +6` at a fifth; **all five were aliases inside `mov`/`jarl`/`dispose`**. On a short 2-byte pattern the hit rate was 5 of 6 WRONG. Pin every new encoder to a **boundary-confirmed** Ghidra instance, or by **three-way field decomposition** if none exists — and assert the near-miss (`sar` vs `shr`, `ld.h` vs `ld.hu`, `ld.h` 0x39 vs `st.h` 0x3B). ⚠ Different failure mode from `search_instructions`, which under/over-counts *analysed* instructions; a byte scan **hallucinates** them. ⊕ **Second, distinct trap in the same file:** `hw1` bit 5 is an `ld.bu`/`ld.hu` displacement's **bit 0**, but on `ld.h` (op 0x39) it is **just the opcode's low bit** — same position, opposite meaning. Encoding `-0x67ac` with it set reads **`gp-0x67ab`**, a real adjacent cell: a plausible wrong answer, not a crash. Always reconstruct `(hw2 & 0xFFFE) | (hw1 bit 5)` **as a live gate**.
- ★★ [V65 BUILT, UNFLASHED — the saturation ladder](accord-v62-flashed-grinding-is-fixed.md) — V62 + cave payload only; **CAL CRC and 0xD2000 byte-identical to V62**, 56 bytes off V62 (52 cave + MAIN CRC). A 4-level symmetric ladder on the aggregator output `gp-0x6b94` vs its own ±10240 clip: `0xE7` +RAIL / `0xA7` +HALF / `0x87` NEUTRAL / `0x97` −HALF / `0x9F` −RAIL, via one `ld.h` + one `sar 0xc` + 4 rungs, 62/68 bytes. Three decoder-checked invariants (bit6⇒bit5, bit3⇒bit4, **never both sides** — also the discriminator vs V59/V64 byte4). Headline = bit6↔bit3 alternation rate. 🛑 Draft 1 spent 2 bits on `gp-0x67ac`, which is **structurally pinned at 0** (cal `0xC4124` = (0,0,5,0,5,5,0,0,0,5,0), nothing in {2,3,4}) — the V64 mistake forming again; rebuilt. Image `f12171a8…`; RWD `dabc9241…`.
- 🛑 [The V62 "new grinding" is ONE phenomenon with ≥5 instances, and the RATCHET is loudest HANDS-OFF](accord-v62-flashed-grinding-is-fixed.md) — **operator corrected both, data confirmed both.** Ranking engaged windows by 30-49 Hz env99: 10:12:14-15 (3829/3794/3477), **10:23:21-24 (1225/946)**, 10:15:59 (1219), 10:14:18 (886) — all engaged, all under real driver effort, **1.9-17.7 m/s**. The "two different phenomena" split was an artifact of a `|d(tq)|>2000` threshold only the largest crossed; a sibling agent had already found them the same class at 3.7× severity. **Rank by a continuous statistic before classifying.** ⊕ RATCHET 6-12 Hz env99 at creep: V62 **hands-off 2665 (n=25) vs hands-on 392 (n=14) = 6.8×**; V59 1265 vs 506. **The driver's hands DAMP it** — use the hands-off arm as primary; a driver-load gate selects AGAINST it.
- ★★★★ [GRIND #2 IS V62's OWN FIX SEEN AT HIGH FREQUENCY](accord-grind2-is-a-45hz-mode-under-driver-load.md) — corner-conditioned tail maxima, Kd=1x vs Kd=2x, 219 blocks: **1-4 Hz 1.01 · 10-16 Hz 0.80 · 18-22 Hz 0.35 · 24-28 Hz 2.66 · 30-40 Hz 2.98 · 40-49 Hz 11.71 (p=0.0003)** — a **monotone response with a crossover at 22-24 Hz**, driver band flat as a control. **One knob cut grind #1 by 2.9x and raised grind #2 by 11.7x.** Cause: `gp-0x4f62` is a **4-sample finite difference**, so its gain RISES with frequency (1.93x at 41.6 vs 20.9 Hz) and V62's *flat* x2 is not frequency-selective. Grind #2 = **~44.9 Hz, Q≈37**, NOT a harmonic (slope 0.173 [-0.92,1.59] vs 2.0), **NOT LKAS-gated** (p99 1.33x vs grind #1's 6.63x), lives at creep under `tq_avg` 1600-2700. ✅ **Confirmed on the comma IMU** — 20-50x baseline during bursts, ρ 0.23-0.55, p≪1e-70 ⇒ a real whole-car vibration on a sensor independent of the EPS. 🛑 Frequency is **aliased and unresolved** (CAN ~100.5 Hz grid; IMU ~101 Hz is only 0.5 Hz away so it does NOT break the degeneracy). 🛑 **A filter cannot fix it** — differentiator +20 dB/dec vs one pole −20 dB/dec is FLAT above the corner; two poles cost −92° at 20.9 Hz and destroy the fix. Separation must come from an operating condition: **driver torque separates them >8x**, steering rate only ~2x.
- ★★ [The dead gp-0x683c gate is a free conditional-gain arm](accord-gp683c-dead-gate-is-a-free-lkas-arm.md) — **ONE access image-wide** (`ld.bu -0x683c[gp],r15` @`0x3AA94`) and **ZERO writers**, raw-scanned in both encodings; its only consumer is `setfne lp` @`0x3AAA8`, and `lp` selects cal `0xC6446` for r24 and `0xC6444` for r26 (**one reader each**). ⇒ repointing that single load is a **ONE-BYTE edit** (`0x3AA96`, even displacements only) that makes the rate-lane gain conditional on any gp byte cell — the only conditional-gain surface here needing **no code cave**. 🛑 Chatter is the kill criterion (a gain switching at the mode frequency is a parametric pump); 🛑 `gp-0x671d` OUTRANKS the arm and would mask it.
- ★ [r24's gain_B uses FOUR pointer arrays, not four consecutive records](accord-r24-gain-b-four-pointer-arrays.md) — `0xCBF5C`/`0xCC044`/`0xCC12C`/`0xCC214` at `mode*4`; mode 10 ⇒ `0xD2A74`/`0xD2AB0`/`0xD2AEC`/`0xD2B28`. Reading them **consecutively** from `0xD2AEC` picks up mode 11's interleaved rows and **understates the rolloff by 2x**. At creep the gain is **3072** (not the oft-quoted 2305, which is the 50 km/h record), flat to motor rate 400, falling to 1536 at 3000. gain_A (r26) is **not** mode-indexed. ⚠ `build_v62_tva.py`'s tripwire watches only 2 of the 4 records.
- 🛑 [Report the MEAN and the TAIL together](feedback-mean-and-tail-must-be-reported-together.md) — the same data gave **0.913 [0.791, 1.026]** (a confident null) on matched-cell means and **27/219 vs 1/91 blocks** (a confident positive) on the extreme tail. The matched q99 threshold was **317** against burst amplitudes of **3000-4000** — the mean test was structurally blind. Print tail thresholds next to event amplitudes; mark **provoked** routes (a route driven to demonstrate a symptom cannot evidence its rate); report exposure **in seconds** in the conditioning corner.
- ★★★ [The aggregator NEVER rails — the loop is LINEAR there](accord-aggregator-never-rails-loop-is-linear.md) — V65's ladder on `gp-0x6b94`, **120,049 frames**: liveness 100%, zero invariant violations, **+RAIL 0 / −RAIL 0**, only **54** frames past ±4096, `bit6↔bit3` alternation **0.0000/s in every arm** (no rail frame exists, so no flip sequence exists). The sum never comes within 20% of its ±10240 clip ⇒ **no describing-function/saturation reasoning is needed anywhere in this chain, and a lane-gain change propagates faithfully** — which is why V62's flat ×2 produced its band table. ★ All 54 non-neutral frames sit **inside grind #2 bursts** at 36–106× the median 30-49 Hz envelope. 🛑 **DO NOT apply V65's pre-committed "all four quiet ⇒ NOT another lane gain" clause to grind #2** — that branch tested the RATCHET as a rail-to-rail limit cycle, and grind #2's attribution rests on an on-car **dose-response on exactly a lane gain**. An intervention outranks an inference from a different hypothesis. What it DOES close: the ratchet cannot be amplitude-saturated *at the aggregator*, and the `0xD2AEC` breakpoint lever loses its clipping rationale. ⚠ 100 Hz sampling a ~43 Hz burst is stroboscopic — quote the route-wide ±8192 null, never the 54 as a rate. ✅ V65 flight-clean, **ST==4 = 0** both routes (recounted off raw CAN) ⇒ zero-EME streak extends.
- ★★★ [gp-0x6806 IS the LKAS gate, validated on-car](accord-gp6806-is-the-lkas-gate-validated-on-car.md) — V57's probe measured it in July and nobody correlated it. **99.90–99.94% agreement with `carControl.latActive`** over **37,914 frames** on two routes at very different duty (21.7%/49.9%), and **0.03–0.05 transitions/s**. ✅ It IS "LKAS is applying"; ✅ it does NOT drop out during steady engaged holding (the one ambiguity static analysis could not close — the ramp-FSM "settled" phases simply do not occur mid-drive); ✅ the parametric-pump kill criterion passes by **three orders of magnitude**. ⇒ V67's gate is closed BEFORE the flash. 🛑 The lesson is where to look: the answer sat in `_cache_r28`/`_cache_r29` since July while every session treated it as an open Ghidra question. **Check whether a past probe already flew on a signal before tracing it.**
- ★★★★ [V67 FLEW: both grinds fixed, and the highway symptom is NOT the rate lane](accord-v67-flew-both-grinds-fixed.md) — route `47`, 150,327 frames. Probe live: byte4 = {0x87,0xC7}, **bit6 == latActive 99.983%**, bit5/bit4 = **0 in every frame** ⇒ clean binary arm. **Grind #1 fixed with a WITHIN-ROUTE gate A/B**: engaged arm **0.524 [0.337, 0.804]** vs Kd=1, disengaged arm **1.055 [0.669, 1.354]** ⇒ suppression in ONE arm only (also the first evidence ever separating V66 from V67). **Creep grind #2 ELIMINATED**: 0 bursts vs Kd=2's 24, max 84 vs 1831 — 🛑 manual arm solid (P(0)=0.020), **engaged arm UNRESOLVED (22 s, P(0)=0.35)**. 🛑🛑 **The 2.44×-at-highway prediction was REFUTED**: three-dose highway null — 40–49 Hz **0.970 [0.787, 1.154]** and **0.938 [0.764, 1.184]** vs null [0.73, 1.37], manoeuvre-conditioned 0.999 / 0.884, and the **corpus-max highway envelope (851.5) is on the STOCK Kd=1.00 lane**. ✅ Positive control: 18–22 Hz IS suppressed at highway on Kd=2 (**0.509 [0.39, 0.92]**). ⚠ 10–16 Hz is **wheel order 1**, not a dose effect. 🛑 The ratchet lead is **CLOSED negative** (f0 9.13 Hz, moves to 11.13 in cruise, 15 Hz lock absent). ✅✅ **The microphone — no frequency ceiling, validated 4.14× on grind #2 — also sees nothing** (1.067×; the stock-Kd route r2b gives 1.071×), bounding the highway event at **≤~9% of grind #2's absolute acoustic amplitude**. Flight-clean, `ST==4` 0/150,327. ⇒ **KEEP V67; no control change supported.**
- ★★★ [r24's gain is a two-axis SPEED × RATE surface — and the units were got WRONG once](accord-r24-gain-is-a-speed-rate-surface.md) — cross axis `0xC6010` = 0/10/50/100 km/h, inner axis `gp-0x6ac0`. 🛑🛑 **RETRACTED the same night: “bus = 8 × deg/s”, “the rate axis is dead” and “V67's arm delivers 1.94×” were ALL WRONG.** Settled two ways: regressing `rate_c` on the differentiated ANGLE channel gives slope **0.95–1.00, r ≥ 0.985** ⇒ the bus field IS deg/s; and at 4.7121 counts/deg-s the breakpoints are **85/297/637 deg/s**, which real driving reaches (max **521 deg/s** over 407,617 frames), whereas the wrong scale puts them at 679/2377/5093 where Honda's rolloff could never engage. ⇒ **V67's build note was CORRECT (LERP 2622 ⇒ exactly 2.00×)**, and the rate axis IS usable: grind #1 ~603, creep grind #2 ~1206 (both on the `[400,1400]` rolloff), highway ~141–198 (X1 = `0x0190` exactly, and Y0 == Y1 in every curve **except** mode-10's 50 km/h record `0xD2AEC` (2305 vs 2304 — a +1 rounding artifact, 0.04%, behaviourally nil, but an exact equality test breaks on it)). **The error was composing two unverified structural relations into a scale instead of measuring it.** ★ **Design A** — one halfword, `0xD2ABC` 2561→7051 — gives grind #1 **2.00×**, creep grind #2 **1.22×**, highway **1.00×**; blast radius clean two ways, no float mirror, CRC block #41 only. Not recommended while V67 already has both at zero.
- 🛑🛑 [Every VIBRATION instrument is blind above ~50 Hz — but the MICROPHONE is not](accord-both-instruments-blind-above-50hz.md) — CAN **100.000 Hz exactly** (Nyquist **50.00**), comma IMU **101.02 Hz** (Nyquist **50.51**, settled by a lattice fit 77 µs vs 2889 µs and a synthetic fold test where **7/7 tones fold per 101.02**; the earlier 99.9–100.5 came from the dt *mean*, which ~1% dropped samples inflate). ★ Headroom is the **wrong quantity** for the alias: the discriminant is a **1.021 Hz** apparent-peak difference and the measured sem is **0.856** where ≪0.34 is needed ⇒ needs a log at a different IMU ODR (208/416 Hz). ★★ **The MICROPHONE has no ~50 Hz ceiling** (`soundPressure` — ⚠ **corrected 2026-08-03**: one RMS over 1600 samples of 16 kHz PCM ⇒ **0–8000 Hz analysed**, level at 10.000 Hz; this line previously said "audio at 16–48 kHz", which makes the channel look more capable than it is and causes its null to be **over-weighted** — the **26.4 dB** bandwidth-penalty argument depends on the band being 0–8 kHz) and a **working positive control**: creep grind #2 reads **4.14×** un-weighted p95 and **+9.7 dB(A)** burst-vs-quiet. ★★ It is also no longer the only above-50 Hz instrument — `gp-0x671a`'s input is a band-pass **peaking at ~61 Hz**, see [[accord-state671a-is-an-oscillation-detector]]. 🛑 A-weighting is the trap (−30 dB at 50 Hz) — use the **un-weighted** channel; *un-weighted up with A-weighted flat* IS the low-frequency signature.
- 🛑 [Check the data exists before concluding it doesn't](feedback-check-the-data-exists-before-concluding-it-doesnt.md) — two sessions asserted "no Kd=1 highway baseline exists"; route `2b` had **227 s** of it, and finding it refuted a confident, arithmetically-correct prediction. A scoping claim inherited from a previous session is **not evidence** — re-derive it. Same failure as V57's probe sitting unread for a month. **When your own arithmetic makes a tidy story with the operator's report, look HARDER for the disconfirming dataset.**

- 🛑 [No speed/torque byte exists to gate on, and the >50 Hz probe is dead at the cave site](accord-no-speed-byte-exists-in-this-firmware.md) — two search passes: every candidate is multi-valued, inline-only, standstill-only, dead (`0xC62EA`=0 since V53), or answers the same “LKAS applying” question V67 already found insufficient. Architectural reason: this firmware's idiom for speed is **“always LERP, never threshold-and-latch”**. 🛑 **Do NOT repoint the mask arm `gp-0x671d`** — a rising-edge counter driving **DTC 0x5e**, read by **8 functions** incl. 4 reads in the motor-off dispatcher `FUN_0003d4a2`; unlike the dead `gp-0x683c` it is a LIVE fault response. 🛑🛑 The hook `0x55C0E` runs at **100 Hz (task 5)**, NOT 1 kHz, and **no stock writer ever clears bits 7:3** of `gp-0x1514` (8 accesses, all masked RMW) ⇒ a sticky HF latch could never clear and the cave cannot see 1 kHz content at all. ⚠ **`gp-0x67ac` OPEN**: when it is 1, r24/r26 drop out of the aggregate entirely, regardless of gain arm.
- 🛑 [A hardcoded SEGS_2B list hid the only Kd=1 highway baseline for three sessions](accord-segs2b-bug-hid-the-kd1-highway-baseline.md) — `_r31_common.SEGS_2B` **and** `_r37_ratchet_lib.ROUTES` both enumerate route `2b` as `[0,1,2,11,12,13]`, but it has **14 segments and 3–10 are the highway leg** = **227 s of Kd=1.00 highway**, the only stock-rate-lane highway exposure in the corpus. That absence is what let a confident wrong prediction stand unrefuted. **A hardcoded subset in a helper module is invisible to every analysis that imports it** — the code runs and the numbers look reasonable. Count exposure from the caches, not through a route table: `r47_orchestrator_checks.py exposure`.
- ⚠ [Two measurement-instrument corrections that move published frequencies](accord-segs2b-bug-hid-the-kd1-highway-baseline.md) — **`fs_of()` is biased +0.5–1.4% route-dependently**; the true `0x14A` rate is **100.000 Hz on every route**, so grind #2's long-quoted "44.9 Hz" is **44.6 Hz** and the between-route frequency spread was the instrument, not the car. And **`0x18F`'s opendbc factor 0.1 is wrong — it should be 0.125** (`rate_f/rate_c` measured 0.756–0.799 ≈ 0.80 on four routes). Analyses using `rate_c` alone are unaffected.
- 🛑 **SUPERSEDED 2026-08-03 by [there is NO line at all in 30–49.5 Hz at highway](accord-highway-30-49hz-has-no-line.md)** — the order-3 half of the line below is an **estimator tautology**: `order = f0·CIRC/v` returns ≈3.00 whenever a band-limited argmax sits near the centre of 30–49.5 Hz at the corpus's median ~28 m/s, *whatever the spectrum contains*. Replaced by: peak of the **averaged** periodogram reads prominence **1.23–3.83** across 30–49.5 Hz on every route, build and channel, against the kit's **>4** criterion — no line, so not order 3 either. ⚠ **The 10–16 Hz order-1 half STANDS** (prominence up to **79**, order 1.00–1.02 in every speed bin), and so does the general warning. Original claim, kept visible:
- 🛑 [At highway, 40–49 Hz is WHEEL ORDER 3 and 10–16 Hz is ORDER 1](accord-v67-flew-both-grinds-fixed.md) — measured per-window order p50 **2.994** and **1.995** (n>600); at 30.8 m/s order 3 = **44.3 Hz, one bin from grind #2**. ⇒ **anyone peak-finding 40–49 Hz on a highway log will "find grind #2" and it will be a tyre.** The bursts themselves are NOT the order (on/off-order power ratio 6.94 quiet → **0.82 in bursts**). Order 1 explains the only band that reads outside the null in the three-dose table (10–16 Hz at 1.55/1.50) — tyre balance between drives months apart, **not** a dose effect. Second time this kit has nearly published a wheel order as a firmware effect.
- 🛑 [Ground EVERY session in the golden model AND the post-V38 arc](feedback_ground_sessions_in_golden_model_and_post_v38_arc.md) — operator instruction 2026-08-03; `eps_lkas_chain_model.py` end-to-end **plus** the whole V38→present record read as one arc, not the latest handoff. Prime every subagent with both. Direction matters: V39/V42/V61 all pushed the rate lane DOWN, the gradient pointed UP.

- 🛑🛑 [There is NO spectral line at all in 30–49.5 Hz at highway — order 3 RETIRED](accord-highway-30-49hz-has-no-line.md) — the recorded *"40–49 Hz is wheel order 3, per-window order p50 **2.994**"* is an **arithmetic tautology**: `order = f0·CIRC/v` returns ≈3.00 whenever a band-limited argmax sits near the centre of 30–49.5 Hz at ~28 m/s. Replaced by the **averaged**-periodogram peak: prominence **1.32–3.83** (bar), **1.23–2.13** (`ay`), **1.26–1.76** (`gz`) against the kit's **>4** criterion — every route, every build, every channel. ✅ The instrument WOULD see a line: the 8–30 Hz positive control returns wheel order 1 at **prominence 15–79**, free-order fit **1.07**, slope **+0.4836 [+0.4806, +0.4863]** vs order 1's 0.4808. Event `f0` vs speed **+0.1139 [−0.0539, +0.3072]** excludes orders 1/2/3/4; on/off-order power **7.24 quiet → 1.97 at event peaks**. ⚠ **10–16 Hz order 1 still stands.**
- ★★★ [The highway 40–49 Hz null survives an EVENT-RATE re-test — and here is the power](accord-highway-event-rate-null-with-power.md) — the pooled-median null was challenged as blind to rare bursts, so the machinery that found creep grind #2 was pointed at highway. **Same null**: event-rate ratios **0.855 [0.432, 1.702]** and **1.152 [0.496, 2.690]** vs a split-half null of **[0.36, 2.50]**, **min detectable 1.61×** at 80 % power; monotone-rising in **no** band, and at 40–49 Hz the max-dose build has the *lowest* rate. ✅ Positive control: grind #1's own event rate falls monotonically with dose (**0.565 [0.329, 0.984]** → **0.319 [0.130, 0.661]**). ⇒ two independent statistics, one answer — the earlier null was **not** a statistic-choice artefact. 🛑 **0.0 s of LKAS-off driving above 12 m/s exists** (verified via `cc_lat` **and** `0x18F` b4 bit3) ⇒ "only when engaged" is **untestable by construction**; `cruiseState` would fake a 123–187 s arm. Bound: a fixed-pitch mode **above ~50 Hz is invisible to both channels**.
- ★★ [The highway "events" are the smooth loading effect's top tail, not a threshold](accord-highway-events-are-the-loading-tail.md) — the operator reported threshold-like; **it is not.** P(event) rises through **every** decile of steering rate from the 5th up (0.7 → 6.4 → 22.1 → **61.2 %**, ρ +0.420) with no zero-then-jump, and envelope-vs-`rate_pk` ρ **+0.654 / +0.705 / +0.721** reproduces the recorded +0.64…+0.93. Trigger is a **~1.5 s steering-rate transient** (median |rate| 1.0 → 5.0 → 6.5 → **18.0** → 5.0 deg/s) at constant speed. ✅ **Hands-off confirmed 19/20**, **rail duty 0.00 on every event**, duration 0.22–0.87 s. 🛑 **No closed-loop signature** — command→bar coherence **0.169** in event windows vs **0.166** background, against grind #1's 0.917 at 21.09 Hz. ⚠ A 2-component fit "prefers bimodal" (ΔBIC 174) — don't read it; the high component is **42 % of windows at 2.4×**.
- 🛑 [A median-of-per-window-argmax MANUFACTURES a line at band centre](feedback-average-periodograms-before-peak-finding.md) — it reported a fixed **~42 Hz mode** on four IMU axes across 22→35 m/s and beat "wheel order 3" by **ΔBIC 249–460**; it matched the operator's reported fixed pitch; **it was an artefact and was withdrawn.** With no line present the argmax scatters and its median lands at band centre (39.75 Hz for a 30–49.5 band). **Fix: average the periodograms FIRST, then find the peak** — the mode vanished, prominence 1.23–3.83. Same class: the `order = f0·k/v` tautology. ⇒ **never test with a ratio built from a band-limited estimate divided by the variable under test — use a slope with a CI or a binned table**, and always run a positive control through the identical pipeline. Also killed here: the **CVT/engine-order** alternative for a fixed pitch (rpm 1330–2400, corr(rpm,v) only **+0.270**, required slope **−0.0333 Hz/rpm** vs measured **−0.00071 [−0.00251, +0.00084]**). ⚠ `argsort(argsort(x))` is not a tie-aware rank — it gave ρ **+0.393** against a monotonically *falling* decile table.
- 🛑 [Route 47 owns 79.8 % of the corpus above 28 m/s — dose is confounded 1:1 with route there](accord-route47-owns-the-fast-highway-exposure.md) — 623.8 s of 781.2 s; **Kd=1 holds 39.2 s (5.0 %)**, Kd=2 118.3 s. So any >28 m/s dose claim is a **one-route-per-arm** comparison across different road, tyres, weather and date, and a within-route split-half null cannot see that. It bit once already: an IMU 41–44 Hz ratio of **1.484 [1.295, 1.617]** looked decisive until the pre-declared **33–36 Hz negative control moved with it (1.185)**. Only between-route control at constant dose: `r37` vs `r3b` = **0.958 [0.836, 1.112]**, one degree of freedom. ⚠ Route `4a` is V67 but has **zero highway** (max 13.92 m/s). ⇒ **~4 min of engaged >28 m/s on a non-V67 build** closes this and the power gap.

- ★★★ [The mic's TWO WEIGHTINGS are a two-point FILTER BANK — grind #2's sound is NOT all at 40–49 Hz](accord-mic-two-weightings-are-a-filter-bank.md) — A-weighting is **−32.41 dB at 44.6 Hz** but **−19.14 dB at 100 Hz**, so `soundPressure` ÷ `soundPressureWeighted` reports *where energy sits* although neither channel can name a frequency. On 10 demonstrated creep bursts the **A-weighted channel rose MORE** (6.51× vs 4.59×) ⇒ excess mean A-weight **4.28× [2.28, 9.86]** that of a pure 44.6 Hz excess ⇒ **effective centroid 63.5 Hz [54.2, 79.6]** — the whole interval **above the 50 Hz ceiling**, and on `gp-0x6c2c`'s band-pass peak (61 Hz). First data ever to place grind #2 energy above the ceiling; independent of the firmware arithmetic that set V68's threshold. Robust to statistic (5.64/3.07/3.31); mic fires on 8 of 10 and tracks bar magnitude. 🛑 **MEAN-WEIGHT inversion — it proves the excess is not all sub-50 Hz, it does NOT locate the energy**; harmonics at 89/134/178 Hz are BELIEF. ⚠ Tyre scrub not excluded (partial ρ **+0.507 [+0.297, +0.634]**, n=2956, survives |rate|/effort/speed control). ⚠ A reviewed centroid of "95.5 Hz [66.8, 170.5]" **does not reproduce** — that maps to 18.29×, not 4.28×.
- ★★ [Grind #1 is a TORSIONAL COLUMN MODE; grind #2 reaches the chassis](accord-grind1-is-torsional-grind2-reaches-the-chassis.md) — two independent estimators on the same events. **Level:** grind #2 clears its null on **every** IMU axis (bar 77.1 / `ay` 58.8 / `gz` 36.2 / `gy` 21.3 / `ax` 20.1 / `az` 19.2 / `gx` 11.4, mic 4.59); grind #1 clears on **NONE** over 48 events (1.45–2.10, all inside null) while the bar reads 12.87. **Coherence** (scale-free): grind #2 **0.82–0.88 every axis** vs 0.30–0.61 control; grind #1 **no contrast anywhere**. ⇒ that is **why the IMU never showed grind #1's reduction** — it was never coupled to it, at any frequency, independently of the 50 Hz ceiling. Axes identified from data (`ax` vertical, `az` ρ −0.839 vs dv/dt, `gx` ρ +0.975 vs v·steer = yaw, `gy` pitch / `gz` roll). BELIEF: lateral ≫ roll > pitch ≈ vertical ≫ yaw reads as a **lateral rack/subframe force with a roll couple**, not wheel-hop, not yaw.
- 🛑 [The mic read 1.061 on grind #1 — inside its null — on a large REAL oscillation](accord-mic-negative-carries-almost-nothing.md) — the cleanest demonstration of its blind spot in the corpus: the bar reads **12.87× [9.0, 14.9]** on the same 48 events. **Rule: a mic POSITIVE is informative** (its creep grind #2 control **replicates at 4.59× [2.95, 8.31]** vs the 4.14× on record, different estimator *and* different control design, and via the A/un-weighted contrast a positive carries real spectral information); **a mic NEGATIVE on a TACTILE event carries almost nothing.** Never read "the mic saw nothing" as "there was no vibration" — it means "nothing this instrument can hear."
- 🛑 [Three timing constants: the CAN rate, the 115 ms mic delay, and the ±50 ms lead/lag floor](accord-three-timing-instrument-constants.md) — **(1) `1/median(dt)` is the WRONG CAN rate**: frames are timestamped **per log packet**, so on r47 12 % of `dt` exceed 15 ms and p10 is exactly 0, and `median(dt)` reads **100.76 Hz** on a grid that is **100.000 Hz to 2e-5**. Use the mean rate + an index lattice; timestamps wander **7.5–10.3 ms** = the CAN alignment uncertainty. **(2) The microphone pipeline delay is 115 ms**, MEASURED against road impacts (35 segments, peak ρ 0.512); `micd.py` alone predicts 75 — the extra **~40 ms is audio-capture buffering**. Subtract it from any sound↔CAN alignment. **(3) Accel and gyro carry SEPARATE hardware-timestamp offsets** (`a_off_sd` 1.47–2.19 ms): bar→IMU lags read +45/+80 ms on bursts but **+90/+40 ms on road excitation with no grind — same magnitudes, order swapped** ⇒ **±50 ms is the empirical floor and NO physical ordering is resolvable** (true transit 0.2–3 ms, 30–500× below the finest step). Use **coherence**, not lag, to test causation here.
- ⚠ [The highway acoustic budget: a quantified BOUND, because the test cannot bear the weight](accord-highway-acoustic-budget-bound.md) — κ = **0.0091 [0.0059, 0.0159]** (log–log slope 0.72 [0.39, 0.96], R² 0.83) calibrated on creep bursts predicts a highway acoustic excess of **8.5–12.7 %** against a measured mic floor of **19.3–109.9 %** ⇒ the prediction sits **2–9× UNDER the instrument** on every route. **A highway acoustic null is therefore uninformative**, and the honest output is the bound: unexplained acoustic energy up to **2–9×** the sub-50 Hz-implied amount would be invisible. Three assumptions, each failing in a known direction (radiation efficiency ~f² vs a 9.9× louder ambient — partly cancelling, **neither measured**; a torsional highway mode would be invisible to the IMU even at creep; the floor is a steady-tone floor, a <5 Hz modulated event is ~5× easier). ⚠ **The joint detector is MIC-LIMITED** — the mic is the minimum channel in **97 %** of burst blocks ⇒ **"joint" buys SPECIFICITY, not sensitivity**; its reach is the *weakest* channel's. Highway counts 1/0/1/4, exact Poisson intervals **all overlap**.
- ⚠ [The highway tri-channel coincidence is REAL but DOSE-INDEPENDENT — it does NOT revive the rate lane](accord-highway-trichannel-coincidence-is-dose-independent.md) — top-decile 40–49 Hz highway blocks vs a circular-shift null: the chassis clears on **4/4** routes (`ay` 1.347 / 1.866 / 1.372 / 1.437, `gz` 1.348–1.608) and the mic on **2/4**. So the joint view *does* see something the single views underweighted. **But it is not monotone in dose** and the **stock Kd = 1.00 lane is not the lowest** ⇒ it is the already-characterised **manoeuvre-loading tail**, a property of car and driver, not firmware. The rate lane has now returned null at highway under **three** independent statistics — pooled level, event rate, and tri-channel coincidence — with both positive controls firing. A fourth channel did not change the answer, only the characterisation.
- 🛑 [A re-cut under the same build number DESTROYS its predecessor's plain image](accord-recut-overwrites-the-previous-plain-image.md) — **OPEN HAZARD; the fix is recommended, NOT applied.** Two V70 cuts 19 min apart both wrote `_v70_plain_image.bin`, so the second silently **overwrote** the first's snapshot while the first's `.rwd` stayed **flashable** ⇒ **an artefact existed that NO gate could check** (`verify_v70_image.py` asserts the *current* topology so it **fails on the old build by construction**; `diff_build_vs_stock.py` has no image to read). ⚠ Its bytes are documented only because they were read **inside the 19-minute window** — luck, not process. ✅ The flash risk was closed by renaming it `SUPERSEDED-DO-NOT-FLASH-…` (`9d44efc`); 🛑 **the verifiability hazard is not.** **FIX (not done): write `_v<NN><tag>_plain_image.bin`, or refuse to overwrite a snapshot whose SHA differs.** ⚠ The old image **cannot be trivially regenerated** (its builder config is gone); recovery by decoding the `.rwd` **was not attempted**. ★ Companion: **`bit6 ⇒ bit3` is build-CLASS identity, never FILE identity** — re-cuts share a byte-identical cave, so **the filename is the only pre-drive discriminator.**
- ★★★ [V70's 4-bit SIGN probe — the durable part of V70](accord-v70-built-sign-probe.md) — 🛑 **no SHAs/build status in that memory on purpose: V70 has been RE-CUT and its hashes, filename and control path change with each cut** (they live in `docs/STATE.md`'s "V70 — BEING RE-CUT"); **the probe does not change.** ★★ 68 of 68 cave bytes, zero spare: bit6 `gp-0x6ada >= +512` · **bit5 `gp-0x67fa == 10` (THE STATE GATE)** · bit4 `gp-0x6adc >= 0` (r26 SIGN) · bit3 `gp-0x6ada >= 0` (r24 SIGN). ★ **Build-CLASS identity from the value set alone — a first**: bit3 is guaranteed non-constant ⇒ invariant **bit6 ⇒ bit3**, only 12 of 16 payloads reachable ⇒ **V53/V54/V65–V69 excluded absolutely**, incl. the build on the car; ⚠ residual **V55/V57/V58/V64** filename-only. 🛑 **It cannot separate two V70 cuts from each other — build-CLASS identity is not FILE identity.** 🛑 bit4 is the SIGN not a `+512` threshold (cave 2 B short; a `>=+512` null was the *predicted* outcome) ⇒ **r26 LIVENESS, not the quantitative `a`.** ★ bit3 is amplitude-independent ⇒ **bit6 = ratchet SIZE, bit3 = PRESENCE.** 📋 Pre-registered: **bit5 reads LOW.** 🛑 **A SUPERSEDED FIRST V70 `.rwd` (`…LKASGATED-V68CONTROLPATH…`) IS STILL IN THE FLASH DIRECTORY** with the OPPOSITE control path and a **byte-identical cave** ⇒ the probe cannot separate them on-car; **rename it `SUPERSEDED-DO-NOT-FLASH-…`.** ⚠ It is also unverifiable now — both cuts write `_v70_plain_image.bin`, so the newer one **overwrote** the older image. ⚠ **Lesson: a fixed `_vNN_plain_image.bin` silently destroys the previous cut of the same version.**
- ★★★ [The ratchet, first real characterisation](accord-ratchet-characterised-on-route-4f.md) — **46 windows / 118 s at ≥1200 counts p-p, peak 6,065**; seg 1 a **continuous 20 s** event, seg 0 one more — **the operator's "mostly segments 0 and 1" confirmed exactly, called before the data.** Median **7.79 Hz** (zero-crossing, FFT-free) / 7.56 Hz (spectral), matching the recorded 7.56 ± 0.36. Order veto: slope **+0.0358 Hz per m/s** vs order 1's 0.482 ⇒ speed-invariant; rpm 773–1724 with f0 static ⇒ not an engine order. ★ **NEW: the ~7.5 Hz line is in the BAR and the ANGLE-RATE but NOT in openpilot's COMMAND** (`e4tq` 6–9 Hz prominence 2.7 vs a threshold of 10, holding at rail duty ≤2%) ⇒ **the loop closes inside the EPS + plant; openpilot is not the oscillator.** **44/46 engaged, 0 manual**, Fisher **p = 4.6e-5**, matched **6.65 [2.45, 12.85]**. ⚠ **Widen "creep only"** — one 4,843-count episode at **12.7 m/s**. ⚠ **Q is NOT measurable at NFFT 256** (cap ~13.3) ⇒ the recorded Q ≈ 36 is neither confirmed nor refuted. ⚠ **The "flat-topped/saturated" premise is NOT what `4f` shows** — crest **2.07–2.45** vs 1.414 for a sine, no flat-topping on any filter ⇒ BELIEF-level re-framing. r24 dose ladder 0×→4×: **NULL and under-powered** (negative control itself 0.38–2.47). ⚠ **Route `4a` cannot speak to the ratchet** — hands-off cell is 13.0 s with zero episodes.
- ★★★★ [`gp-0x67fa` state-gates the assist chain — and state 10 SPLITS IT IN HALF](accord-gp67fa-state-gate-on-assist-chain.md) — **[EVIDENCE, instruction level, `FUN_0002214a` `0x2214a`–`0x22a84`]**, index a plain `1 << (gp-0x67fa & 0xf)`, no off-by-one. **The guard wraps the `jarl` in the CALLER**, and each callee has exactly one call site ⇒ in a masked-out state the function is **never invoked at all** — no stack frame, 0% of body. **THREE masks:** `0x221d6` **`0x830` = {4,5,11}** → `FUN_00036388` + **`FUN_000428d4` (the OSC DETECTOR)**; `0x22518` **`0x930` = {4,5,8,11}** → `FUN_00028ea6`/`FUN_0002b422`/`FUN_0002b57a` (**ARBITRATION = `gp-0x6806`'s producer**); `0x2269a` **`0xc30` = {4,5,10,11}** → `FUN_0003a382` + **`FUN_0003aa2c` (THE AGGREGATOR)**. ⇒ **in state 10 the aggregator and the residual lane RUN while the detector, return-to-centre AND arbitration DO NOT — assist is delivered from a stale `gp-0x6806`.** ★ **State 10 is REACHABLE in normal operation**: written twice in `FUN_00019970` (the state-4 handler) — `0x199CC` (diagnostic, `tp+0x74d0 == 0xa`) and **`0x19A72` (NORMAL)**, gated on bit 15 of `gp-0x6d78` with bit 16 → state 11 taking priority; writer set over 33 `st.b` sites (Ghidra and a raw byte scan agree exactly) = {1,3,4,5,6,7,8,9,10,11}. **[OPEN] what bit 15 means** — that decides how *often*, not whether. 🛑 **This is a LIVE ALTERNATIVE for the five-build detector null** ("never CALLED" has the identical signature to "ran and found nothing"). ⚠ **BUT V67's OWN PROBE ARGUES AGAINST IT, and must be quoted alongside**: state 10 is absent from `0x930` too, so `gp-0x6806` would go **stale** there — yet V67 measured `gp-0x6806` == `latActive` in **150,302/150,327 = 99.983%**, all 25 disagreements single-frame edges. **A stale flag cannot track transitions that closely** ⇒ **the ECU is predominantly NOT in state 10 while engaged and the nulls are probably GENUINE** [BELIEF, indirect]. ✅ **V70's bit5 rung (`gp-0x67fa == 10`) settles it, non-vacuous both ways**: ≈ 0 ⇒ the nulls are genuine and five builds vindicated; materially non-zero ⇒ the nulls were on the gate. ⚠ **The detector has a SECOND entry gate, still OPEN** — `FUN_00046ea6(5)` on bit 5 of `gp-0x18d0`/`gp-0x18d4`; the record's earlier closure established only that that **function** has one caller, **not** that the **bit** is clear in operation. 🛑 **Bus `STEER_STATUS` is NOT `gp-0x67fa`** (`4f` reads ST=0 on 47,990/47,990 while steering, and state 0 is in no mask) — any reasoning that equated them is invalid. ⚠ **Provenance:** decompiled against stock `code.bin` with the 33 writer sites byte-identical in `_v68_plain_image.bin`; the **dispatcher itself was not decompiled from a V68/V69 image** — BELIEF by adjacency, not EVIDENCE. ⚠ `get_xrefs_to` returned a false "No references found" here, and a `jarl` Format-V scanner mask bug (bits 15:11 are **reg2**; `disp = ((hw1 & 0x3F) << 16) | hw2` sign-extended from 22 bits) gave zero hits for functions Ghidra had just given callers for.
- ★★ [The ~28 Hz lane-change transient is DOSE-INDEPENDENT](accord-lane-change-transient-is-dose-independent.md) — V69's stated purpose **failed**: the transient **survived and is LARGER in p-p on V69** (2,599 / 4,094 counts vs V68's 1,468), and **it runs at full amplitude on the STOCK rate lane** (V58/r2b at 1.000× → 2,389 counts p-p @27.59 Hz; **V59/r2c at 1.000× carries the corpus's largest, 3,283 @27.07 Hz**). Non-monotone — V62 at 2.000× is *quieter* than V58 at 1.000×. Pooled speed-matched **2.000×/1.000× = 1.176 [0.641, 2.320]** (inside null), **2.403×/1.000× = 2.897 [1.271, 11.439]** (does not clear), route-level Theil-Sen slope on dose **+5.736 [−25.432, +34.934]** — **0 inside**. ★ **Excitation, not gain, is the live candidate**: within dose = 1.000× exactly, ALC vs driver-commanded = **2.389 [1.453, 4.898]** (null [0.44, 2.26], does not clear, one manual route), and holding excitation fixed collapsed the 2.403× contrast **2.849 → 2.013** with the CI crossing 1 — *"an excitation contrast wearing a dose label"*, the same class as the withdrawn 28 Hz "mode". ⇒ 🛑 **V70 must NOT chase the rate lane for this symptom.**
- 🛑 [Size a probe rung against the LANE's own reachable output, not a downstream gate width](feedback-size-probe-rungs-against-lane-reachable-output.md) — V69 spent **all three rungs** and got nothing, and the failure was **arithmetic**. **bit4 (`gp-0x6ad4` ≥ +4096) was STRUCTURALLY VACUOUS — it could never have fired on any build, any drive**: `±0x2800` is the **ERR *input* clamp**, while the output is clamped to **±CEILING = MIN of three LERPs**, the binding one `0xC67C2`/`0xC67C8` indexed on **voted vehicle speed**, **max 1024**, starting at **ZERO** — at the ratchet episodes' speeds CEILING was **164–341**, so the test sat **12–25× above the lane's entire reachable range**. ★ Also explains **why V56's mute of this lane changed nothing**. **bit5 was insensitive not vacuous** (reachable max 5786 ⇒ 4096 = 71% of range, saw only the top 29%); **bit6 had no exposure** (replay predicts ~1 hit, observed 0, p ≈ 0.37 — not the V64 gate failure, but not a positive control either). ⇒ **compute the producing lane's reachable range at the operating point and state it next to the threshold**; budget a probe like a cave — **enable + raw input + a rung whose range you have computed**. Companion to [[feedback-probe-the-gate-not-just-the-output]]: that one is *which signal*, this one is *what value*.

- 🛑🛑 [Monitor 2 mechanics + the `tp+0x74a4 = 0xC64A4` trap (**made THREE TIMES** — third occurrence 2026-08-06)](reference-accord-monitor2-corridor-and-the-c64a4-trap.md) — **RULE: never byte-check a tp-relative address you were handed; recompute `tp+disp` and show the addition.** ⊕ **Re-verified 2026-08-06: `0xC64A4 = 0x00` on STOCK, V74 AND V75 ⇒ Monitor 2 is ARMED in every build**; the wrong `0xC74A4` reads `0xEA`. 🛑 And its **magnitude sizing is REFUTED** — the faulting V75 launch was the MILDEST of four, so the "maximum-demand, minimum-speed corner" story is dead; only the edge-vs-dwell *structure* survives. Reading `0xC74A4` (=0xEA) instead of `0xC64A4` (=0x00) declared Monitor 2 "permanently gated off"; it is **ARMED in every build**, and the orchestrator relayed the error by verifying the wrong address. Mechanics: ±5/1024 on `gp-0x6b98`, charge **+0.001**/leak **−0.0005** ⇒ **break-even duty exactly 1/3**, trip at 0.01 ⇒ **10 consecutive 1 kHz cycles = 10 ms → DTC 0x1c/0x1d → `0xF00049`, latched**. ★ **Sign alternation does NOT cancel** (bilateral `|diff|` flags); **no minimum-duration gate**. ★ The corridor compares `gp-0x6b04` (PRE-clamp) vs `gp-0x6b98` (POST) ⇒ **equal unless a clamp BINDS** — the physical reason a transition trips it and a dwell cannot. ✅ `gp-0x6dac` CLOSED (driver-torque sensor voting, 1 writer/1 reader, two methods) — not the path.
- ★★★★ [LKAS "friction" is VISCOUS DAMPING, not a rate limit — and V74 made it engaged-only](reference-accord-lkas-friction-is-viscous-not-a-rate-limit.md) — the operator was right; "it's an openpilot safety feature" was wrong. 🛑 **No live hard rate limiter exists anywhere** (`0xC61D6`=0 disabled, `0xC6194` dead, 45-site `gp-0x6b98` sweep finds only magnitude clamps). Three viscous terms oppose column rate; the always-there baseline is **`gp-0x6b26` (`FUN_00036c12`), VIRGIN — never edited, strongest at 0 km/h**. 🛑 **No term is indexed by commanded torque — `FactorB` is flat unity in modes 24 AND 26**; opposition is mediated through MOTION, never demand. ⊕ On stock, modes 24/26 carry **byte-identical** friction and damping ⇒ **the LKAS-vs-manual asymmetry now on the car is OURS**: mode-26 friction ×1.5 since V74 (manual byte-stock), plus `0xC407E` 511→850 since V73 which is **not** mode-indexed and hits manual too. Levers ranked; friction ×1.5 was already in force on V74 which did NOT fix the grind ⇒ reverting it is the clean single-variable feel test.

## 2026-08-06 session — V74 hard-faulted in MANUAL; V75's fault pinned to one frame; V77 built

- ★★★★★ [V74 HARD-FAULTED in MANUAL, over a bump](accord-v74-hard-faulted-in-manual-over-a-bump.md) — latched total loss of power steering, **LKAS DISENGAGED**; lamp on continuously, still on after engine restart, out after ~30 s of driving (classic DTC maturation: fault set with `warningIndicatorRequested` → assist latched off → power cycle restores assist but not the lamp → monitor re-runs clean → indicator dropped). 🛑 **[EVIDENCE, orchestrator-verified TWICE] the FactorC/FactorE edits were NOT in force** — disengaged = **mode 24**, and all five mode-24 damper records are **byte-identical to stock on V74 AND V75** (FactorC `0xD67E4` `X=[2240,3840,5120,8960] Y=[0,234,429,908]`, FactorE `0xD6820` `X=[60,400,2500,4000] Y=[0,140,539,927]`, FactorB `0xD6760`, FactorD `0xD67A4`, ceiling `0xD60B4`); independently **0 of the 54 non-CRC V73→V74 diff runs** land inside any mode-24 record. 🛑🛑 ⇒ **`k* ∈ (0.580, 1.580]` is VOID and "V74 flew 1,011 s clean" is no longer a safety anchor** — every gain-margin argument in the V75 analysis rested on it. **No build in the current lineage has demonstrated safety.** ★ Live in manual and non-stock: `0xC63A0`=2048 (V72), `0xC407E`=850 (V73), `0xC61B2/B4`=2048 (V38), boost floor 5120/5.0 (V38), `0xC62EA`=0 — **none is new at V74** (V72/V73 carried the same manual config with no manual fault) ⇒ **n=1**, and the trigger may simply be the first sufficient bump.
- ★★★★★ [V75's fault is PINNED TO ONE FRAME](accord-v75-fault-pinned-to-the-frame.md) — route `5e` (`75604b0a432fdc89_0000005e--857d0bd164`), 7 segments, 401.2 s. Fault at **t = 284.7947 s, segment 4**, one 100 Hz frame, everything latching together: bus `STEER_STATUS` (`gp-0x6807`) 0→**7**; `STEER_CONTROL_ACTIVE` 1→0; `gp-0x6880` 0→1 (0 for the whole prior drive); **`0x1AB` byte0 bit2, the firmware's own DTC-active flag, 0→1**; `0x14A` STEER_ANGLE/ANGLE_RATE/WHEEL_ANGLE **all → `0x7FFF`**; `STEER_SENSOR_STATUS` 7→4. openpilot reacted +5 ms; dash-lamp frames on `0x450`/`0x440` at +0.12/+0.28/+0.52 s. ★★ **The faulting launch was the MILDEST of four engaged stoplight launches** — #2 sat on openpilot's ±4096 rail **76%** of its window and drove the damper to a higher bracket without faulting; #4 had **0.00% rail contact** and the lowest driver torque ⇒ 🛑 **magnitude and rail contact are NOT the discriminator; every magnitude-based mechanism is dead.** ★★ The damper never approached its ceiling — the `≥448` probe bit fired **0 / 39,961 frames**. ★★ 300 ms pre-fault: a **20.0 Hz oscillation** — 1,368 ct p-p driver torque, 93 ct p-p angle rate, dominant line 20.0 Hz on both, **absent from openpilot's command (3.3 Hz)** — the kit's own ~21 Hz plant mode; the damper thermometer stepped up one bracket 20 ms before, then froze for 116 s. ★ Post-fault the control task is **ALIVE** (probe cave still executing, 2,165 `gp-0x6ac2` transitions) and CAN is alive (0x14A/0x18F both exactly 100.0 Hz) with MOTOR_TORQUE frozen ⇒ **motor-off latch, not a task death or a reset**; driver effort median rose **12×**.
- ★★★★ [Descriptor **bit13** is the fault fingerprint](accord-descriptor-bit13-is-the-fault-fingerprint.md) — [EVIDENCE, orchestrator-verified in Ghidra] `FUN_00040a50` forces the `0x14A` angle fields to `0x7FFF` when `FUN_00040906(1)==0xff && FUN_00046ea6(0xd)!=0`. `FUN_00046ea6(N)` = **bit N of the OR-aggregate `(gp-0x18d0 | gp-0x18d4)`**, which ORs the first word of the fault-descriptor record at **`tp-0x72bc = 0xBF000-0x72BC = 0xB7D44`, stride `0x1c`, fault_id 0–125**; `0xd` = **bit13**. ⇒ **the angle sentinel and `STEER_SENSOR_STATUS` 7→4 are CONSEQUENCES of a bit13 fault, not a cause — no re-pointing to the angle domain is warranted.** And bit13 is a **fingerprint**: it **RULES OUT** fid 4 (`0x00001C01`, init self-test), fid 80 = `0xC41668` (`0x00000C00`, ADC timeout), fid 72 = `0xD48394` (`0x00000C20`); it **RULES IN** the category-`0x2D`/`0x3D` ids, including **fid 28 (Monitor 1)** and **fid 29 (Monitor 2 / `FUN_00045a20`)**, both `0x00003D01`. ⚠ **fault_id numbering trap** — the direct map at `tp-0x5AB8 = 0xB9548` (stride 4, DTC = raw bytes[2],[1],[0]) gives `0xC41668`=**80**, `0xD48394`=**72**, `0x540011`=**16**, one lower than several agent reports claimed.
- 🛑🛑 [The DTC read is STRUCTURALLY BLIND here](accord-dtc-read-is-structurally-blind-here.md) — **`0xF00049` is a catch-all shared by ~42 distinct fault_ids**; its status byte cannot identify which monitor fired even in principle. Worse, a multi-member group's UDS status is **not an OR across members**: the display picks a winner from a **live RAM** fault-log array (pointer `tp-0x7fcc`) by priority, falling back to the group's first ROM member (**fid 4**, a power-on self-test) — and **the RAM log is cleared by the power cycle** ⇒ **a fresh `0x1c`/`0x1d` trip is invisible in a `19 02` read taken after a restart.** ★ Provenance [EVIDENCE, from raw ISO-TP]: `flashing-2020accord/dtc_script_output_stock_eps_preV21new.txt` shows `5902ce 540011 40 c41668 08 d48394 40 f00049 48 f00055 40` on **stock firmware before this kit's first flash**; today's read differs in exactly two bits (`0xC41668` gained pendingDTC, `0xD48394` gained confirmedDTC) and **`0xF00049` is byte-identical to stock**. `dtc_script_output_2.txt` shows `f00049 = 0x0E` when it genuinely fires, so the store IS responsive. 🛑 **`0x23` ReadMemoryByAddress is not implemented on this ECU** (NRC `0x11` in all three captures, three firmware eras). ⇒ **retires "the decisive outstanding measurement is a DTC read."**
- ★★★★ [Path 2 is a CLOSED firmware loop, and `0xC63A0` weights it](accord-path2-is-a-closed-firmware-loop-and-c63a0-weights-it.md) — `gp-0x6bd0` reaches the motor by TWO routes. **Path 1** = `FUN_0003aa2c`, the aggregator, **unity weight, zero phase** — this is what delivers the damping. **Path 2** = `FUN_00038148` stage 1: **six gated terms, plain ADD, no subtraction**, each `(x * gate * w) >> 10`, then × polarity `gp-0x6752` × `tp+0x7468 = 0xC6468 = 2639` >>10, then a 1 kHz IIR `state += ((16*target − state) * (tp+0x73ac = 0xC63AC = 102)) >> 10` (**corner 16.70 Hz**) → stage 2 → `gp-0x6b70` → `FUN_00037fe6` → `gp-0x6ad6` → `FUN_0003a382` (PID) → back into the aggregator → `gp-0x6b98`. ★★ **`gp-0x6b98` RE-ENTERS Path 2 one sample later via `FUN_0003b8f6`** ⇒ **Path 2 is a closed feedback loop inside the FIRMWARE, not through the plant**; `FUN_0003b8f6` runs at `0x2240e`, before the governor at `0x229ce`, in the same 1 kHz tick — a clean one-sample delay, not an algebraic loop. 🛑 **`0xC63A0` does not touch the `gp-0x6b98` re-entry term at all, and that term may dominate. OPEN, highest-value next trace.** ★★ **`0xC63A0` = `tp+0x73a0`, u16 Q10, is the ODD ONE OUT of six sibling weights** (`0xC63A0`..`0xC63AA`, all stock **1024**): it is **the only one any build has ever moved** — V72 set it to **2048** and no build reverted it until V77. **Mode-proof bare `tp` scalar ⇒ live in MANUAL and ENGAGED.** 1 reader (`0x381AC`), 0 writers, **no monitor, no float mirror** (two-method null). - 🛑🛑 [`0x18F`'s payload is ONE CAN FRAME (≈10 ms) stale vs `0x14A`'s](accord-0x18f-payload-one-frame-stale.md) — **corrects every `command → bar` phase this kit has ever computed**, by 99° at 27.5 Hz. Derived from a first-principles invariant: the transmitted rate is the derivative of the transmitted angle, so it must lead by +90°, and a lead-error constant in **ms** (not degrees) is a pure timebase offset — `rate_c`/`0x14A` **−2.2 ms** vs `rate_f`/`0x18F` **−12.1 ms**, difference = one frame. ⚠ `rate_f`'s stored scale is 0.8× the truth (use ×1.25); `cs_tq`/`cs_rate` are unusable above ~10 Hz (corr 0.60 at 27.5 Hz); 1.7% of CAN rows share a `logMonoTime` and silently NaN any `np.gradient` over `t`.
- ★★★★ [Aliasing IS resolvable: `|Rate(f0)|/|Ang(f0)| = 2π·F_true` survives the fold](accord-alias-resolution-via-derivative-ratio.md) — the EPS differentiates at 1 kHz **before** the 100 Hz sampling, so the fold hits angle and rate identically and the magnitude ratio still reports the TRUE frequency. **Closes an ambiguity the kit carried through a dozen handoffs as unresolvable.** Route 67: measured 119.2 rad/s vs 171.8 (27.34 Hz) / 456.5 (72.66 Hz) ⇒ **the 72.66 Hz twin is EXCLUDED at 3.8×**. - ★★★★★ [RESOLVED: the aggregator DOES reach the motor — via the `gp-0x6acc` bridge](accord-aggregator-reaches-motor-via-gp6acc-bridge.md) — `gp-0x6b94` → governor `FUN_0004503c` → `gp-0x6ace` → comp-add `FUN_000456a4` → **`gp-0x6acc`** → shaper reads it @`0x431C4` → `gp-0x6b08` @`0x43206` → SM2/SM3 integrator → `gp-0x6b98` → FOC → PWM. **Orchestrator byte-verified the crux**: predicted encodings `244f3495` / `645ff894` both MATCH, writer `st.h r8,-0x6acc,gp` @`0x45932`, mode cal `0xC64C8` = **0x00 (pass-through) on stock and every build**. 🛑 **Eleven methods missed it because they all asked "who reads `gp-0x6b94`" — nobody asked about `gp-0x6acc`, two hops out**; and `gp-0x6b08`'s "self-referential, no external reader" characterisation was true and misleading (the function's OWN next instructions consume it). ⊕ The chain was **already documented** in `reference_accord_post_governor_comp_add.md` and never cross-checked against the newer "cannot reach" claim. **Explains V40's brick mechanistically** (removing the governor slew snaps `gp-0x6ace` to target → unbounded step into the integrator) **and the graded V74→V81 dose-response** (dose in, dose out, no threshold) ⇒ the DTC-side-channel hypothesis is **SUPERSEDED**. 🛑 `0xC64C8` mode 1 **discards** the aggregator for a static cal, mode 2 blends — a one-byte switch, UNTESTED, writer census not run. ⚠ Last hop `0x43226`→`gp-0x6b98` still inherited, not hand-walked. **Corrects `accord-v77-cannot-reach-the-monitors.md`.**
- 🛑🛑 [A bandpass ringdown Q is meaningless without a STEP CONTROL](accord-ringdown-q-needs-a-step-control.md) — **two agents independently fitted a filter's own step response and read it as plant damping** on the same route-67 edge (Q≈7 and Q≈10.4–10.8 at **R² 0.987**; a filter's step response IS a clean exponential, so high R² proves nothing). Bandpassing a *perfect step* with zero plant decay yields apparent **Q 9.0–11.6**; widening the band 8× shows no separation from that control. ⇒ **Q ≲ 6 upper bound; the 27.5 Hz ring STOPS (~1.8 cycles), it does not decay** ⇒ **not a forced resonance by anything** — every "drive × Q" bound is void — but an **actively sustained limit cycle with an engaged-only source**, and only openpilot's command (share ≲3–5%) and the engaged-only damper switch at that edge. 🛑 No cross-build Q ladder is obtainable (a gateless fitter returned Q=1028/tau 11.8 s); every disengage edge measures the **DISENGAGED** plant (mode 24, byte-stock on V76/V80/V81). ⚠ Full-event linewidth Q=103 is f₀ wander (**0.576 Hz**), not damping — which also breaks harmonic fold arithmetic for N≥5 (±4 Hz smear at 7f); use a drift-tolerant phase-lock test instead.
- 🛑🛑 [~~FactorD is the ANGLE-ERROR lever — the only frequency-selective one~~ **HEADLINE REFUTED 2026-08-09**](accord-factord-is-the-angle-error-lever.md) — **`gp-0x6a10` is ABSOLUTE STEERING ANGLE, not a tracking error** (99.94%, step on the threshold's own value, holds in the MANUAL arm) ⇒ **"the only frequency-selective lever" is REFUTED — THIS FIRMWARE HAS NONE**, which also removes the argument that FactorE cannot do what FactorD can. **And FactorD is STRUCTURALLY INERT where the symptoms live**: FactorC multiplies in BEFORE it with `X[0]`=34.97 km/h, `Y[0]`=0, in all four modes. See [[accord-levers-killed-2026-08-09]]. **The disassembly, the +0x0C layout rule and the blast-radius work below are still correct.** ⚠ ORIGINAL TEXT FOLLOWS: `gp-0x6a10` = unsigned, unclamped **angle-tracking-error magnitude at 0.1°/count** (anchored two independent ways, not from FactorD's own breakpoints); X `[0,50,100,150,700]` = **0/5/10/15/70°**; gate `< 0x2711` is an overflow rail. **Flat-unity in every mode, never written by any build, n=5 already ⇒ no code edit needed.** ★ Structurally special: angle amplitude scales as 1/ω, so at 7.79 Hz the excursion is **3.6× larger** than at 27.75 Hz ⇒ **a FactorD rising with angle error preferentially damps LOW frequencies.** That is the only frequency selectivity in this firmware — FactorE cannot do it, because the damper's nonlinearity is **memoryless** ⇒ shape changes magnitude at every frequency identically and never phase. 🛑 Two measurements gate it: `gp-0x6a10`'s real excursion during a ring (column angle p-p 1.29° ⇒ ~6 counts, likely deep in the flat segment) and **`gp-0x67fe` ∈ {1,2}, which is NOT settled**. 🛑 `build_v30_tva.py`'s "driver torque `gp-0x69ca`" was WRONG — corrected 2026-08-08. ✅ **REINFORCED 2026-08-08**: flat unity verified over **2,924 records (86 images × 34 modes)**; layout rule **`X` @`base+2`, `Y` @`base+2+2·npt`** = `+0x0A` for 4-point B/C/E but **`+0x0C` for 5-point D** (a 4-point parse misreads `X[4]=700` as `Y[0]`); excursion **6.71 ct @27.75 Hz · 9.31 @20 Hz · 23.89 @7.79 Hz** ⇒ all inside the first flat segment ⇒ **`X` must be RESHAPED, not just `Y`**; sole table reader is `FUN_00034350` (the other 7 `gp-0x6a10` consumers read the raw cell) ⇒ **fault-isolated**.

- ★★★★ [The CAN telemetry surface, settled empirically — 1.66 M frames](accord-can-telemetry-surface-census-1p66m-frames.md) — census over **1,662,374 `0x14A` frames, 30 routes, 29 builds**, two independent implementations agreeing exactly. ✅ **`0x14A` is PROVEN BYTE-TRANSPARENT** by a counterfactual: 93.2% of frames carry non-zero magprobe bits at positions **no DBC defines**, and a signal-list re-serialiser would have emitted a wrong checksum in **85.5%** of them — it never does. ✅ **`0x14A byte7[7:6]` is free AND survives to the wire** (both stock RMWs of `gp-0x1511` preserve 7:6 — `andi 0xcf` @`0x55BFC` pre-hook, `andi 0xf0` @`0x55C24` **post**-hook); **`byte7[5:4]` is NOT free** (`gp-0x6880 & 3` @`0x55BF6`/`0x55BFA`). 🛑 **`0x18F byte5[4]` is CONFIRMED LIVE ON THE WIRE** — 14,472 frames, but **only** on routes `5e`/`61` (the hard-fault flights), coincident with `STEER_STATUS`→7; flat zero on 28 of 30 routes ⇒ **the canonical proof that empirical-zero ≠ free** (`0x1AB byte0[1]` is worse: 27 frames in 831,200). 🛑 **`0x18F` DLC = 7 in 100% of frames — THERE IS NO BYTE 7**; `byte6[6]` is the last usable bit. ⚠ **`0x18F`'s transparency does NOT transfer from `0x14A`** [BELIEF] — settle it with one build writing a known non-stock pattern into `0x18F byte4[2:0]` plus a live `0x14A` write as an in-flight positive control. 🛑 **`0x1AB` `MOTOR_TORQUE` VARIES ⇒ forbidden as a channel.** Clean-tier total **16 bits**, matching the existing ledger exactly by a different method.
- 🛑 [Two V850 cave-encoding traps, both caught by build-time gates](accord-two-cave-encoding-traps-sar-floor-and-opcode-bit.md) — (i) **`sar` floors toward −∞**, so a naive `cmp -0x4`/`bgt` rung fires at r24 ≤ **−769**, not −1024 — a **255-count asymmetry**; correct immediates are `cmp 0x4`/`blt` → +1024 and `cmp -0x5`/`bgt` → −1025, found by an **exhaustive self-check over all 16,385 values of r24 in ±8192**. (ii) **`ld.h` op `0x39` vs `st.h` op `0x3B` differ by ONE BIT**, and for `gp-0x6ada` the firmware's only other instance of that displacement **is** the store. ⚠ Also: **`gp-0x6a10` is word-aligned**, so an `ld.w` slip would **not fault** — it would silently read `gp-0x6a10` and `gp-0x6a0e` as one word.

- [Gentle EME fires on a saturated LKAS command](gentle-eme-fires-on-saturated-lkas-command.md) — root cause relocated to the debounce SM (fixed at V37); visible only via raw CAN `399`, not `carState`.
- ★★★★★ [V87 BUILT: the first SUBTRACTIVE build](accord-v87-built-measurement-on-v38-base.md) — V38 base + V57 gain + V42 ratchet fix + steer-to-zero + **2 bytes** repointing 427 `MOTOR_TORQUE` to `|gp-0x6b98|`. 🛑 Will read as a NULL by design; the feel change is the REBASE, not an 8 Hz lever.
- 🛑 [`0xC63B8`'s 8 Hz band-pass is a RECTIFIED boost index, not a damper](accord-c63b8-8hz-bandpass-is-a-rectified-boost-index.md) — real structure (peak 8.13 Hz, Q 0.501, untouched in 88 images) but refuted **five ways**; already flown NULL as V60. ⊕ Excellent free *sensor* (`gp-0x6de8`, 1w/0r).
- 🛑🛑 [A filter in the shaper DISABLES THE POWER STEERING](accord-shaper-float-twin-blocks-filter-insertion.md) — `FUN_00043e44` float twin, ±5 counts, 10 ms → DTC 0xF00049. The two "best" hook sites are inside its coverage; phase budget there is **2.4°**. Only `0x453e0` is monitor-clean.
